# --- contract_authority_effects ---
# Kefreyt contract authority.

VAL_initialize_contract_authority = {
	# The ordinary idea is a bookmark preview; active campaigns use one dynamic spirit.
	remove_ideas = VAL_mercenary_state
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_CIN_influence } } set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_OSF_influence } } set_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_APH_influence } } set_variable = { var = VAL_APH_influence value = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_authority } } set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 35 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority min = 0 max = 100 }
	VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
}

VAL_change_contract_authority = {
	VAL_initialize_contract_authority = yes
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority min = 0 max = 100 }
	VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
}

VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_recon_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_special_forces_min value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_volunteer_size value = 2 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_war_stability value = 0.1 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_overall_income_factor value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_admin_expense_factor value = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_fiscal_administration_investment }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_overall_income_factor value = VAL_fiscal_administration_investment }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_fiscal_admin_savings }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_admin_expense_factor value = VAL_fiscal_admin_savings }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_band } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_band value = -1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_new_band value = 5 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 25 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_new_band value = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 50 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_new_band value = 2 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 75 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_new_band value = 3 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 90 compare = less_than } }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_new_band value = 4 }
	}

	# VAL contract band 0-24
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 25 compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = 0.0 }
	}
	# VAL contract band 25-49
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 50 compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.02 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = 0.0 }
	}
	# VAL contract band 50-74
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 75 compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.06 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.10 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.03 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = -0.03 }
	}
	# VAL contract band 75-89
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_authority value = 90 compare = less_than } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = -0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.15 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = -0.05 }
	}
	# VAL contract band 90-100
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.12 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.12 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.07 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = -0.15 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.20 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = -0.05 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = 0.08 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.07 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.07 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = -0.07 }
	}

	# Base mercenary institutions are included once in every recalculation.
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = 0.25 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_contract_payroll_relief }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = VAL_contract_payroll_relief }
	}
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.03 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.02 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = -0.2 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = -0.2 }

	# Recalculate persistent focus bonuses when a focus or authority value changes.
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_conscription_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_command_power_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_consumer_goods_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_tax_collection_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reinforce_rate value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_max_efficiency_factor value = 0.0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_resource_penalty_factor value = 0.0 }

	# Legacy specialization ideas used to carry these stats. Strip them so old saves do not double-count.
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_administration_1
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_administration_2
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_administration_3
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_army_1
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_army_2
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_army_3
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_industry_1
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_industry_2
	remove_ideas = VAL_contract_industry_3

	# Administration specialization is now part of the visible contract-state dynamic modifier.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.06 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_tax_collection_factor value = 0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_state_overload_factor value = -0.08 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_tax_collection_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.03 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_tax_collection_factor value = 0.04 }
	}

	# Army specialization is aggregated into the same dynamic modifier.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_level value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.15 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reinforce_rate value = 0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_command_power_factor value = 0.10 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_level value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reinforce_rate value = 0.01 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_level value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.05 }
	}

	# Industry specialization is aggregated into the same dynamic modifier.
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_industry_level value = 3 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0.12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_max_efficiency_factor value = 0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_resource_penalty_factor value = -0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.08 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_industry_level value = 2 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0.08 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_max_efficiency_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_resource_penalty_factor value = -0.05 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_industry_level value = 1 compare = equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_efficiency_gain_factor value = 0.05 }
	}

	# Successful northern contracts establish permanent broker networks (0-3 per region).
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.06 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.04 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.06 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.04 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_APH_influence value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.06 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_APH_influence value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.04 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_APH_influence value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.02 }
	}

	# Specialised company reforms survive authority changes; reset the base above before adding them.
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_company_rosters }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.06 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_border_survey_complete }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_special_forces_min value = 12 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_recon_factor value = 0.2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_company_service_code }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_war_stability value = 0.2 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contractor_officers }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_motorized_columns }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_field_repair_corps }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.04 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_ministry_auditors }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contract_general_staff }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_volunteer_size value = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contract_nco_schools }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.07 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_provincial_brokers_network }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_export_clearing_house }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.05 }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_captain_retainers_unlocked }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_command_power_factor value = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_negotiation_posture_visible }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_quality_arsenal }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_broad_resource_network }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_supply_contracts }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_doctrine_covert_intervention }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_doctrine_sustained_supply }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = -0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_doctrine_resource_raider }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.04 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_capture_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_defence_factor value = -0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_ash_manpower_preservation }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_conscription_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_ash_rear_mobilization }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = 0.05 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_consumer_goods_factor value = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_north_open_market }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_north_coercive }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = -0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_out_war }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_volunteer_size value = 4 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_paid_loyalty }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_army_expense_factor value = -0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_closed_ledgers }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_state_above_captains }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_command_power_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_captains_cannot_veto }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_front_stories }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_army_of_the_ledger }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_factor value = 0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_planning_factor value = 0.03 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_attack_factor value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contract_throne }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = 0.05 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_weaponry_baron }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_military_income_factor value = 0.03 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_contract_political_mandate }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_pp_gain value = VAL_contract_political_mandate }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_mercenary_state }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_harvest_of_ash }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_stability_factor value = 0.02 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_provincial_courts }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = 0.03 }
	}

    if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_frontier_supply_bonus } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_supply_factor value = VAL_frontier_supply_bonus } }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_frontier_output_bonus } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_factory_output_factor value = VAL_frontier_output_bonus } }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_frontier_trade_bonus } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_trade_income_factor value = VAL_frontier_trade_bonus } }
    if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_frontier_recovery_bonus } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_org_regain value = VAL_frontier_recovery_bonus } }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				check_variable = {
					var = VAL_contract_band
					value = VAL_contract_new_band
					compare = equals
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_band value = VAL_contract_new_band }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_state } }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_state } }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# Run only on startup, outside focus rewards: completion status is stable there.
VAL_migrate_mercenary_payroll = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_payroll_relief } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = 0 }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_State_Contract } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Industrial_Mobilization_Plan } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Army_Of_The_Ledger } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Return_To_World_Market } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_econ_integrated_economy } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_defeat_Repair_The_Workshops } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_defeat_Restore_Freight_Routes } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_defeat_Veterans_Register } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_defeat_Staff_College } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_defeat_Recovered_State } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 } }
		clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief min = -0.25 max = 0 }
	}
}

# Payroll relief belongs to the country and survives replacement of its focus tree.
VAL_reform_mercenary_payroll = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.25 compare = less_than_or_equals } } } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_payroll_reform_tt
	}
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief value = -0.05 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_payroll_relief min = -0.25 max = 0 }
		VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
	}
}

# --- foreign_operation_effects ---
# CIN, OSF and APH contracts.

# VAL export escrow uses one ledger for the shared foreign-operation slot.
# Store measured debits on each producer tag, including absent tags.
# Cross-scope debit measurement lasts until the loop ends.
# Infantry stock preserves producer and quantity; named variants are not tracked.
VAL_reserve_export_rifles = {
	clr_country_flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved
	set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = 25000 }
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Foreign_Broker_Licences } set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = 50000 } }
	if = {
		limit = { OR = {
			AND = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Foreign_Broker_Licences NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 50000 } } }
			AND = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Foreign_Broker_Licences } NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 25000 } } }
		} }
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			# Recheck because the matching producer list can be collected before execution.
			if = {
				limit = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
				VAL = {
					set_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifle_debit value = VAL_export_rifles_remaining }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifle_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_export_rifle_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = VAL_export_rifles_removed }
				}
				if = {
					limit = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_removed value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
					set_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles value = VAL.VAL_export_rifles_removed }
				}
			}
		}
		clear_variable = VAL_export_rifles_removed
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_export_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
			# Freeze the quoted income and paid volume until settlement, including across focus completion.
			if = {
				limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Foreign_Broker_Licences }
				if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Northern_Clearing_House } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value = 40 } }
				else = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value = 30 } }
			}
			else_if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Northern_Clearing_House } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value = 20 } }
			else = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value = 15 } }
		}
		else = { VAL_refund_export_rifles = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY (CIN/OSF): settle the one reserved northern order to its actual buyer.
# PREV within the producer loop restores this recipient; its inner PREV is the producer.
VAL_deliver_export_rifles = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { tag = CIN tag = OSF }
			exists = yes has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
			VAL = { has_country_flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved has_capitulated = no }
		}
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { has_variable = VAL_export_rifles }
			PREV = { add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.VAL_export_rifles producer = PREV } }
			clear_variable = VAL_export_rifles
		}
		VAL = {
			ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
			set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 15 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value > 39 } } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 40 } }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value > 29 } } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 30 } }
			else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved value > 19 } } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 20 } }
			clr_country_flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { tag = CIN }
			VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 2 } clamp_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence min = 0 max = 3 } }
		}
		else = { VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence value = 2 } clamp_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_influence min = 0 max = 3 } } }
		VAL = { VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

VAL_refund_export_rifles = {
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_export_rifles }
		VAL = {
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = PREV.VAL_export_rifles producer = PREV }
		}
		clear_variable = VAL_export_rifles
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAL_export_rifles_reserved
}

VAL_clear_foreign_operation = {
	clr_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_active
	clr_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_deposit
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_study_market
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_arms_brokerage
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_infrastructure_concession
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_hire_local_captain
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_prepare_separate_terms
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_study_market
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_arms_brokerage
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_infrastructure_concession
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_hire_local_captain
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_prepare_separate_terms
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_study_market
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_arms_brokerage
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_infrastructure_concession
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_hire_local_captain
	clr_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_prepare_separate_terms
}

VAL_debit_foreign_operation = {
	add_political_power = -10
	ADISCORD_economy_spend_50 = yes
}

VAL_refund_foreign_operation_deposit = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_deposit }
		ADISCORD_economy_receive_50 = yes
	}
	VAL_clear_foreign_operation = yes
}

VAL_begin_cin_operation = {
	set_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_active
	set_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_deposit
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_study_market
		country_event = { id = val_contract.200 days = 21 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_arms_brokerage
		country_event = { id = val_contract.201 days = 21 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_infrastructure_concession
		country_event = { id = val_contract.202 days = 21 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 compare = equals } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_hire_local_captain
		country_event = { id = val_contract.203 days = 21 }
	}
	else = {
		set_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_prepare_separate_terms
		country_event = { id = val_contract.204 days = 21 }
	}
}

VAL_resolve_cin_operation = {
	VAL_select_cin_contract_posture = yes
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_last_family value = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code compare = equals } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_repeat_streak value = 1 } }
	else = { set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_last_family value = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code } set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_repeat_streak value = 1 } }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_repeat_streak value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 } } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_block_market days = 90 value = 1 } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 } } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_block_arms days = 90 value = 1 } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 } } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_block_infrastructure days = 90 value = 1 } }
		else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 } } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_block_captain days = 90 value = 1 } }
		else = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_block_terms days = 90 value = 1 } }
	}
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_market } } set_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_market add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_arms } } set_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_arms add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_infrastructure } } set_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_infrastructure add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_captain } } set_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_captain add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 } }
	else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 5 } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_terms } } set_country_flag = VAL_CIN_success_terms add_to_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence value = 1 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_influence min = 0 max = 3 }
	VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_CIN_target_cooldown days = 70 value = 1 }
	VAL_clear_foreign_operation = yes
}

}

VAL_select_cin_contract_posture = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_contract_posture value = 0 compare = equals } } random_list = { 30 = { set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_contract_posture value = 1 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 CIN = { has_war = yes } } set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_contract_posture value = 2 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 CIN = { has_stability < 0.4 } } set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_contract_posture value = 3 } } 20 = { modifier = { factor = 2 CIN = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5000 } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_CIN_contract_posture value = 4 } } } }
}

VAL_select_osf_contract_posture = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_contract_posture value = 0 compare = equals } } random_list = { 30 = { set_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_contract_posture value = 1 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 OSF = { has_war = yes } } set_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_contract_posture value = 2 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 OSF = { has_stability < 0.4 } } set_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_contract_posture value = 3 } } 20 = { modifier = { factor = 2 OSF = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5000 } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_OSF_contract_posture value = 4 } } } }
}

VAL_select_aph_contract_posture = {
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_APH_contract_posture value = 0 compare = equals } } random_list = { 30 = { set_variable = { var = VAL_APH_contract_posture value = 1 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 APH = { has_war = yes } } set_variable = { var = VAL_APH_contract_posture value = 2 } } 25 = { modifier = { factor = 2 APH = { has_stability < 0.4 } } set_variable = { var = VAL_APH_contract_posture value = 3 } } 20 = { modifier = { factor = 2 APH = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 5000 } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_APH_contract_posture value = 4 } } } }
}

VAL_resolve_active_cin_operation = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_study_market } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 } VAL_resolve_cin_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_arms_brokerage } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 } VAL_resolve_cin_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_infrastructure_concession } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 } VAL_resolve_cin_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_hire_local_captain } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 } VAL_resolve_cin_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_CIN_prepare_separate_terms } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 5 } VAL_resolve_cin_operation = yes }
}

VAL_resolve_active_osf_operation = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_study_market } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 } VAL_resolve_osf_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_arms_brokerage } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 } VAL_resolve_osf_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_infrastructure_concession } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 } VAL_resolve_osf_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_hire_local_captain } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 } VAL_resolve_osf_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_OSF_prepare_separate_terms } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 5 } VAL_resolve_osf_operation = yes }
}

VAL_resolve_active_aph_operation = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_study_market } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 1 } VAL_resolve_aph_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_arms_brokerage } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 2 } VAL_resolve_aph_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_infrastructure_concession } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 3 } VAL_resolve_aph_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_hire_local_captain } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 4 } VAL_resolve_aph_operation = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_operation_APH_prepare_separate_terms } set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_family_code value = 5 } VAL_resolve_aph_operation = yes }
}

# COUNTRY recipient. Timeout is decline; a stale event cannot close a later offer.
# COUNTRY recipient: receipt survives offer expiry and is consumed once.
# COUNTRY recipient. Capture the cause before refund and offer cleanup erase its evidence.
VAL_capture_partner_failure = {
 set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 1 }
 if = { limit = { OR = { exists = no VAL = { exists = no } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 2 } }
 else_if = { limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes VAL = { has_capitulated = yes } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 3 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 4 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 5 } }
 else_if = { limit = { OR = { AND = { has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade } } AND = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire } } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 6 } }
 else_if = { limit = { VAL = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending } } } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 7 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire has_war = yes } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 8 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire VAL_partner_hire_reserve_ready = no } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 9 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade VAL_partner_trade_terms_valid = no } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 10 } }
 else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire VAL_partner_hire_terms_valid = no } set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_failure_reason value = 11 } }
 log = "VAL contract failure: recipient=[THIS.GetTag] reason=[?VAL_partner_failure_reason]"
}

VAL_refund_trade_fee = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid }
  VAL_capture_partner_failure = yes
  VAL_record_partner_refusal = yes
  clr_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid
  VAL = { add_political_power = 100 clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending country_event = { id = val_contract.425 days = 1 } }
 }
}
# COUNTRY recipient. A lost timed marker must still resolve the escrow exactly
# once and tell VAL that the quote expired.
VAL_reconcile_stale_trade_fee = {
	VAL_refund_trade_fee = yes
}
VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade }
				exists = no
				has_capitulated = yes
				has_war_with = VAL
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes VAL_partner_trade_can_accept = yes }
			VAL_settle_partner_trade = yes
		}
		else = {
			VAL_reconcile_stale_trade_fee = yes
			clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade
		}
	}
}
VAL_reconcile_trade_fees = {
	CIN = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
	OSF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
	APH = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
	COF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
	TFF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
	YPR = { VAL_reconcile_partner_trade_fee = yes }
}

VAL_close_partner_offer = {
	# Compatibility dispatcher for callers without a contract-specific outcome.
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade } VAL_close_partner_trade_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid } VAL_close_partner_trade_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire } VAL_close_partner_hire_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arms } VAL_close_partner_arms_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_bulk } VAL_close_partner_bulk_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arsenal } VAL_close_partner_arsenal_offer = yes }
	else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_strategic } VAL_close_partner_strategic_offer = yes }
}
VAL_close_partner_trade_offer = { VAL_refund_trade_fee = yes clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }
VAL_close_partner_hire_offer = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }
VAL_close_partner_arms_offer = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arms VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }
VAL_close_partner_bulk_offer = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_bulk VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }
VAL_close_partner_arsenal_offer = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arsenal VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }
VAL_close_partner_strategic_offer = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_strategic VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending } }

# COUNTRY VAL. Rebuild income from persistent contract flags rather than ideas that the engine can invalidate.
VAL_recalculate_market_contract_income = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_CIN_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 5 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_OSF_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 8 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_APH_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 2 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_COF_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 10 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_TFF_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 12 } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_YPR_active } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income value = 15 } }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_market_weekly_income min = 0 max = 52 }
	VAL_refresh_market_contract_modifier = yes
}
VAL_reconcile_market_contract_lifecycle = {
	set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_CIN_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = CIN CIN = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_CIN_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { CIN = { exists = yes } } CIN = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_OSF_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = OSF OSF = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_OSF_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { OSF = { exists = yes } } OSF = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_APH_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = APH APH = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_APH_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { APH = { exists = yes } } APH = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_COF_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = COF COF = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_COF_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { COF = { exists = yes } } COF = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_TFF_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = TFF TFF = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_TFF_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { TFF = { exists = yes } } TFF = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_YPR_active OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = YPR YPR = { OR = { exists = no has_capitulated = yes } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_YPR_active set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 } if = { limit = { YPR = { exists = yes } } YPR = { VAL_end_partner_market_income = yes } } }
	if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_market_contract_lifecycle_changed value = 1 compare = equals } } VAL_recalculate_market_contract_income = yes }
}
VAL_refresh_market_contract_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_state } }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_state } }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY partner. Keep the counterpart's one-point weekly benefit in a dynamic modifier.
VAL_refresh_partner_market_modifier = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_partner_market_income } }
		force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	}
	else = { add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_partner_market_income } }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}
VAL_end_partner_market_income = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_market_weekly_income value = 0 }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_partner_market_income } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_partner_market_income } }
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# COUNTRY partner. The timed guard preserves the one-year term across saves and reloads.
VAL_start_market_year = {
 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_market_term_started } }
  set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_market_term_started days = 365 }
  if = { limit = { tag = CIN } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_CIN_active } }
  if = { limit = { tag = OSF } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_OSF_active } }
  if = { limit = { tag = APH } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_APH_active } }
  if = { limit = { tag = COF } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_COF_active } }
  if = { limit = { tag = TFF } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_TFF_active } }
  if = { limit = { tag = YPR } VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_market_contract_YPR_active } }
  VAL = { VAL_recalculate_market_contract_income = yes }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_partner_market_weekly_income value = 1 }
  VAL_refresh_partner_market_modifier = yes
  VAL = { country_event = { id = val_contract.411 days = 365 } }
 }
}

# COUNTRY partner. A successful transaction starts the annual contact interval.
VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = {
 set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_partner_contact_cooldown days = 365 }
}

VAL_resolve_partner_trade_reply = {
 if = { limit = { VAL_partner_trade_can_accept = yes } VAL_settle_partner_trade = yes }
 else = { VAL_decline_partner_trade = yes }
}
VAL_dispatch_partner_trade_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_partner_trade_can_accept = no }
		VAL_decline_partner_trade = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		VAL_resolve_partner_trade_reply = yes
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = val_contract.362 } }
}
VAL_decline_partner_trade = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid } }
  VAL_capture_partner_failure = yes
  VAL_record_partner_refusal = yes
 }
 VAL_close_partner_trade_offer = yes
}

VAL_settle_partner_trade = {
 if = {
  limit = { VAL_partner_trade_can_accept = yes }
  if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid } clr_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid }
  else = { VAL = { add_political_power = -100 } }
  VAL_start_market_year = yes
  VAL_contract_record_success = yes
  VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
  VAL = { country_event = { id = val_contract.424 days = 1 } }
  VAL_close_partner_trade_offer = yes
 }
}
VAL_dispatch_partner_hire_offer = {
	if = {
		limit = { is_ai = yes }
		VAL_settle_partner_hire = yes
	}
	else = { country_event = { id = val_contract.364 } }
}

# COUNTRY partner. Reserve the quoted fee until consent; only settlement pays the partner.
VAL_reserve_partner_hire_fee = {
 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid } VAL_partner_hire_funds_ready = yes }
  set_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid
  VAL = {
   ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
   subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 }
   ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
  }
 }
}

# COUNTRY recipient. A stale answer must not shorten an accepted contract's annual interval.
VAL_record_partner_refusal = {
 if = {
  limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_trade has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arms has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_bulk has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_arsenal has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_strategic has_country_flag = VAL_order_offer has_country_flag = VAL_military_offer has_country_flag = VAL_transit_offer has_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_advisors has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid has_country_flag = VAL_trade_fee_paid AND = { tag = NAM VAL = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_offer_pending } } AND = { tag = EFL VAL = { has_country_flag = VAL_efl_concession_offer_pending } } } }
  set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_partner_contact_cooldown days = 365 }
 }
}

VAL_decline_partner_hire = {
 if = {
  limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid } }
  VAL_capture_partner_failure = yes
  VAL_record_partner_refusal = yes
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid }
   VAL = {
    add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
    add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
   }
   clr_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid
  }
  # Cancel superseded quotes at their original price before accepting a new tariff.
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid }
   if = { limit = { tag = CIN } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 300 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   if = { limit = { tag = OSF } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 400 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 400 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   if = { limit = { tag = APH } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 200 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 200 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   if = { limit = { tag = COF } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 300 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   if = { limit = { tag = TFF } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 400 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 400 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   if = { limit = { tag = YPR } VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
   clr_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid
  }
  VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending country_event = { id = val_contract.423 days = 1 } }
  clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire
 }
}
VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_paid }
		VAL_decline_partner_hire = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_hire }
				VAL_partner_offer_current = no
			}
		}
		# An AI never withholds consent from a still-valid quote. The escrow is
		# enough to recover the offer if its timed UI marker expired first.
		if = {
			limit = { is_ai = yes VAL_partner_hire_can_accept = yes }
			VAL_settle_partner_hire = yes
		}
		else = { VAL_decline_partner_hire = yes }
	}
}

VAL_reconcile_hire_fees = {
	CIN = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
	OSF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
	APH = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
	COF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
	TFF = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
	YPR = { VAL_reconcile_partner_hire_fee = yes }
}
VAL_settle_partner_hire = {
 if = {
  limit = { VAL_partner_hire_can_accept = yes }
  if = {
   limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid } }
   VAL = {
    ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
    subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
    add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 }
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
   }
  }
  add_manpower = -5000
  ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
  add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
  add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
  ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
  clr_country_flag = VAL_hire_fee_500_paid
  set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_partner_hire_cooldown days = 365 }
  VAL = { add_manpower = 5000 country_event = { id = val_contract.422 days = 1 } }
  VAL_contract_record_success = yes
  VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
  VAL_close_partner_hire_offer = yes
 }
 else = { VAL_decline_partner_hire = yes }
}

# Payment and dispatch use one acceptance branch; the quoted tier never follows later focuses.
VAL_settle_partner_arms = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_partner_arms_can_accept = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
		subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		VAL = {
			ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = 25000 target = ROOT }
			VAL_record_instant_export = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VAL_resource_aid_recipient = yes VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } has_active_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline } }
			VAL = { add_to_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_rifles value = 25000 } VAL_complete_resource_aid = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_export_customer_cooldown days = 365 }
		VAL_contract_record_success = yes
		VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
  VAL_close_partner_arms_offer = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL. The charter consumes only the secured bridgehead; Irem remains VAL.
VAL_form_wasteland_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_opened owns_state = 169 controls_state = 169 is_subject = no has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = ERT } WCA = { exists = no } }
        WCA = {
            transfer_state = 169
            set_capital = { state = 169 remember_old_capital = no }
            inherit_technology = VAL
            set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
            set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
            ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        169 = { add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA }
        set_autonomy = { target = WCA autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
        WCA = { load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_administration_focus keep_completed = yes } }
        VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
}
# COUNTRY defeated ERT/IRT. Settle only the current opponent and fixed map sector.
VAL_settle_wasteland_capitulation = {
    set_global_flag = skip_default_capitulation
    save_event_target_as = VAL_wasteland_defeated
    every_enemy_country = {
        limit = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } }
        white_peace = event_target:VAL_wasteland_defeated
    }
    if = {
        limit = { tag = ERT }
        if = {
            limit = { VAL = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Southern_Expansion VAL_wasteland_administration_ready = yes } }
            167 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 167 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            169 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 169 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            170 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 170 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            171 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 171 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            184 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 184 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            185 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 185 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
            203 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 203 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { VAL = { has_completed_focus = VAL_frontier_return_irem } }
            169 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } VAL = { transfer_state = 169 } set_state_controller_to = VAL } }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { VAL = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Return_Southern_Tsaygen } }
            168 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { is_controlled_by = ERT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } VAL = { transfer_state = 168 } set_state_controller_to = VAL } }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { tag = IRT }
        178 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 178 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        180 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 180 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        181 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 181 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        182 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 182 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        183 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 183 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        193 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 193 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        206 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 206 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
        207 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = IRT OR = { is_controlled_by = IRT controller = { OR = { tag = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } } } } WCA = { transfer_state = 207 } add_core_of = WCA set_state_controller_to = WCA } }
    }
    VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
    WCA = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
}

VAL_receive_contract_grant_300 = {
    custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_contract_grant_300_tt
    hidden_effect = {
        ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 300 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 300 }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
}


VAL_apply_contract_administration_1 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_administration_level }
					check_variable = {
						var = VAL_contract_administration_level
						value = 1
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			add_ideas = VAL_administration_1_preview
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 1 }
			VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
		}
	}
}

VAL_apply_contract_administration_2 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			hidden_trigger = {
				OR = {
					NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_administration_level }
					check_variable = {
						var = VAL_contract_administration_level
						value = 2
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
		}
		effect_tooltip = {
			if = {
				limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 1 compare = equals } } }
				swap_ideas = { remove_idea = VAL_administration_1_preview add_idea = VAL_administration_2_preview }
			}
			else = { add_ideas = VAL_administration_2_preview }
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_administration_level value = 2 }
			VAL_refresh_contract_modifier = yes
		}
	}
}

VAL_refresh_contract_reputation = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		VAL_apply_contract_reputation_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		VAL_apply_contract_reputation_2 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		VAL_apply_contract_reputation_1 = yes
	}
	else = { VAL_apply_contract_reputation_0 = yes }
	hidden_effect = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

VAL_apply_contract_reputation_3 = {
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_0
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_1
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_2
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_3
		add_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_3
	}
}

VAL_apply_contract_reputation_2 = {
	hidden_effect = {
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_0
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_1
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_2
		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_3
		add_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_2
	}
}

	}

		remove_ideas = VAL_contract_reputation_2

	}

# COUNTRY VAL: the focus pays for a fixed formation package, not a stockpile transfer.
VAL_raise_mountain_contractors = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_raise_mountain_contractors_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = VAL
				has_capitulated = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_mountain_contractors_raised }
				any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Kefreyt Mountain Contractors" } }
				division_template = {
					name = "Kefreyt Mountain Contractors"
					template_counter = 110
					division_names_group = VAL_INF_01
					is_locked = yes
					force_allow_recruiting = yes
					regiments = {
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						mountaineers = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			# Explicit staffing avoids taking recruits from the free replacement pool.
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Kefreyt Mountain Contractors\" start_experience_factor = 0.3 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = PREV
					count = 2
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = VAL_mountain_contractors_raised
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL: the focus pays for a fixed formation package, not a stockpile transfer.
VAL_raise_reserve_battalions = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_raise_reserve_battalions_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				tag = VAL
				has_capitulated = no
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_reserve_battalions_raised }
				any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_template = "Kefreyt Reserve Infantry" } }
				division_template = {
					name = "Kefreyt Reserve Infantry"
					template_counter = 3
					division_names_group = VAL_INF_01
					is_locked = yes
					force_allow_recruiting = yes
					regiments = {
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 0 y = 2 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 0 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 1 }
						infantry = { x = 1 y = 2 }
					}
					support = { engineer = { x = 0 y = 0 } }
				}
			}
			# Explicit staffing avoids taking recruits from the free replacement pool.
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				create_unit = {
					division = "division_template = \"Kefreyt Reserve Infantry\" start_experience_factor = 0.2 start_equipment_factor = 1.0 start_manpower_factor = 1.0"
					owner = PREV
					count = 2
					allow_spawning_on_enemy_provs = no
				}
			}
			set_country_flag = VAL_reserve_battalions_raised
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

VAL_start_quarterly_contract_norm = {
	set_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_active
	clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid
	clr_country_flag = VAL_ai_emergency_quartermaster_used
	set_variable = { var = VAL_quarterly_contract_months_elapsed value = 0 }
	activate_mission = VAL_quarterly_contract_deadline
}

# COUNTRY: caller sets positive temp VAL_contract_rifle_cost.
# Debit mixed producer stocks exactly; success is reported only through VAL_contract_rifles_paid.
VAL_pay_contract_rifles = {
	clr_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid
	set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining value = VAL_contract_rifle_cost }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_cost value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = num_equipment@infantry_equipment value = VAL_contract_rifle_cost compare = greater_than_or_equals }
		}
		every_possible_country = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
			PREV = {
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
					set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_debit value = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining }
					multiply_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_debit value = -1 }
					add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_contract_rifle_debit producer = PREV }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_removed value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
					subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining value = VAL_contract_rifles_removed }
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifles_remaining value = 0 compare = equals } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid
			log = "[VAL_CONTRACT] [THIS.GetTag] producer-aware rifle debit=[?VAL_contract_rifle_cost]"
		}
		else = {
			log = "[VAL_CONTRACT] [THIS.GetTag] rifle debit incomplete; remaining=[?VAL_contract_rifles_remaining]"
		}
	}
}

VAL_pay_quarterly_contract_norm = {
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = { VAL_can_pay_quarterly_contract_norm = yes }
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_cost value = 40000 }
			VAL_pay_contract_rifles = yes
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid }
				add_political_power = -10
				set_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid
			}
		}
	}
}

VAL_subcontract_quarterly_norm = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_can_subcontract_quarterly_norm = yes }
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
		set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid value = 2 }
	}
}

VAL_complete_quarterly_contract_norm = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid value > 1 } } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = 1 }
		army_experience = 15
		add_command_power = 15
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_active
	clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level min = 0 max = 3 }
	VAL_refresh_contract_reputation = yes
	country_event = { id = val_rework.1 days = 1 }
}

VAL_fail_quarterly_contract_norm = {
	clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_active
	clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = -1 }
	clamp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level min = 0 max = 3 }
	add_to_variable = { var = VAL_quarterly_contract_failures value = 1 }
	VAL_refresh_contract_reputation = yes
	country_event = { id = val_rework.1 days = 1 }
}

VAL_resolve_quarterly_contract_norm = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_active }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_paid }
			VAL_complete_quarterly_contract_norm = yes
		}
		else = { VAL_fail_quarterly_contract_norm = yes }
	}
}

VAL_offer_westerholm_concession = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_westerholm_offer_tt
	hidden_effect = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				WRK = { exists = yes }
				38 = { is_owned_by = WRK is_controlled_by = WRK }
				NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_concession_granted }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_offer_pending }
			}
			set_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_offer_pending
			WRK = { country_event = { id = val_contract.320 } }
		}
	}
}

VAL_grant_westerholm_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			WRK = { exists = yes }
			38 = { is_owned_by = WRK is_controlled_by = WRK }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_concession_granted }
		}
		38 = { add_resource = { type = steel amount = 10 } }
		WRK = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 38 } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_concession_granted
		set_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_resource_rights_active
		add_ideas = VAL_westerholm_concession_income
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_assert_vorkerland_metal_shortage = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_completed_focus = VAL_New_Supply_Base } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_metal_lost } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_metal_lost
		}
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VAL_vorkerland_metal_shortage } }
			add_ideas = VAL_vorkerland_metal_shortage
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VAL_vorkerland_metal_shortage }
		remove_ideas = VAL_vorkerland_metal_shortage
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_handle_vorkerland_war_outbreak = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		clr_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_offer_pending
		remove_ideas = { VAL_big_buyers VAL_westerholm_concession_income }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps value = 0 }
		VAL_end_vorkerland_resource_access = yes
		VAL_assert_vorkerland_metal_shortage = yes
		set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage value = 0 }
		VAL_refresh_arsenal_reputation = yes
		VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = yes
		country_event = { id = val_rework.100 hours = 1 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_handle_westerholm_loss = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_resource_rights_active }
		remove_resource_rights = 202
		remove_resource_rights = 38
		clr_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_resource_rights_active
		set_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_metal_lost
		remove_ideas = VAL_westerholm_concession_income
		VAL_assert_vorkerland_metal_shortage = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}


VAL_migrate_contract_tier_levels = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_administration_level }
			has_completed_focus = VAL_State_Contract
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_administration_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_administration_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Central_Payment_Office
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Provincial_Contract_Courts
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_administration_2 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_administration_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_The_Weaponry_Baron
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Provincial_Brokers
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Ministry_Auditors
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_administration_1 = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_industry_level }
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Industrial_Mobilization_Plan
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_industry_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_industry_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Standardize_Rifle_Lots
