				has_completed_focus = VAL_Standard_Cartridges
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Three_Shift_Arsenals
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_industry_2 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_industry_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Contract_Accounting_Office
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Munitions_Board
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_industry_1 = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_army_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Contract_General_Staff
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Army_Of_The_Ledger
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_army_3 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_army_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Contractor_Officers
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Motorized_Columns
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Field_Repair_Corps
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Contract_NCO_Schools
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Logistics_Command
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_army_2 = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_army_level }
			OR = {
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Count_The_Captains
				has_completed_focus = VAL_The_Mercenary_State
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Company_Rosters
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Border_Survey_Corps
				has_completed_focus = VAL_Company_Service_Code
			}
		}
		VAL_apply_contract_army_1 = yes
	}
}

VAL_initialize_rework = {
    VAL_initialize_northern_bloc = yes
	VAL_initialize_arsenal_recovery = yes
	VAL_initialize_command_balance = yes
	VAL_initialize_contract_authority = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Operational_Directorate
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_operations_map_unlocked }
		}
		VAL_unlock_operations_map = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Ministry_Of_Contract_Memory
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_propaganda_slot_granted }
		}
		ADISCORD_campaign_slot_grant = yes
		set_country_flag = VAL_propaganda_slot_granted
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Two_Concurrent_Narratives
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_second_propaganda_slot_granted }
		}
		ADISCORD_campaign_slot_grant = yes
		set_country_flag = VAL_second_propaganda_slot_granted
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Quarterly_Contract_Norm
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_contract_reputation_level }
		}
		set_variable = { var = VAL_contract_reputation_level value = 3 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_quarterly_contract_failures value = 0 }
		VAL_refresh_contract_reputation = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_completed_focus = VAL_Quarterly_Contract_Norm
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_contract_active }
				NOT = { has_active_mission = VAL_quarterly_contract_deadline }
			}
		}
		VAL_start_quarterly_contract_norm = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted }
		}
		VAL_handle_vorkerland_war_outbreak = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_westerholm_resource_rights_active
			38 = { NOT = { controller = { tag = WRK } } }
		}
		VAL_handle_westerholm_loss = yes
	}
}

# VAL: debit auxiliary equipment by producer for the accepted full package.
VAL_cw_pay_contract_auxiliary = {
	every_possible_country = {
		limit = { PREV = { OR = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than }
			check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than }
		} } }
		PREV = {
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_removed value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_debit value = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = VAL_cw_contract_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_removed value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = VAL_cw_contract_removed }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_removed value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_debit value = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining }
				multiply_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_debit value = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = VAL_cw_contract_debit producer = PREV }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_removed value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = VAL_cw_contract_removed }
			}
		}
	}
}

# VAL: the pending flag stores package 1/2 for 21 days.
# After STS accepts, recheck both parties before settling resources and payment.
VAL_cw_complete_arms_contract = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			NOT = { has_variable = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles }
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_trade_course
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_refused }
			has_capitulated = no
			NOT = { has_war_with = STS }
			STS = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = STP }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value < 2 }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 32000 } }
					STS = { ADISCORD_economy_can_spend_500 = yes }
				}
				AND = {
					has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 43340 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment < 96 } }
					NOT = { has_equipment = { support_equipment < 60 } }
					NOT = { has_manpower < 12600 }
					STS = { check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 compare = greater_than_or_equals } any_owned_state = { is_controlled_by = PREV } }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_cost value = 32000 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_manpower_debit value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_contract_rifle_cost value = 43340 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_manpower_debit value = -12600 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = 96 }
			set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = 60 }
		}
		# Record stock before debiting to calculate exact refunds on failure.
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_rifle_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_refund value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
		set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_refund value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
		VAL_pay_contract_rifles = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 } }
			VAL_cw_pay_contract_auxiliary = yes
		}
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_rifle_refund value = num_equipment@infantry_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_refund value = num_equipment@ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_refund value = num_equipment@support_equipment }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid
				check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_rifle_refund value = VAL_contract_rifle_cost compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_remaining value = 0 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_remaining value = 0 compare = equals }
			}
			add_manpower = var:VAL_cw_contract_manpower_debit
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_rifles value = VAL_contract_rifle_cost }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract value = 1 }
			if = { limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 } } set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_contract value = 2 } }
			STS = {
				set_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_buyer_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
				if = { limit = { VAL = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 } } } ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes }
				else = { ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes }
				subtract_from_temp_variable = { var = STP_ps_buyer_before value = ADISCORD_economy_treasury }
				VAL = { set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_money value = PREV.STP_ps_buyer_before } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_sequence value = 1 }
			set_variable = { var = STP_ps_val_receipt_stage value = 1 }
			activate_mission = STP_ps_val_departure

			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending value > 1 } }

				set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled value = 2 }
			}
			else = { set_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_contract_fulfilled }
			army_experience = 5
			set_temp_variable = { var = ADISCORD_VAL_effect_value value = 5 }
			VAL_change_contract_authority = yes
			log = "[VAL_CONTRACT] contract settled: rifles=[?VAL_contract_rifle_cost] donor manpower=[?VAL_cw_contract_manpower_debit]"
		}
		else = {
			clr_country_flag = VAL_contract_rifles_paid
			# Refund the measured equipment debit to VAL stock; the offer can be retried.
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_rifle_refund value = 0 compare = greater_than } } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_cw_contract_rifle_refund producer = VAL } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_squad_refund value = 0 compare = greater_than } } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = ADISCORD_squad_weapons_equipment amount = VAL_cw_contract_squad_refund producer = VAL } }
			if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_cw_contract_support_refund value = 0 compare = greater_than } } add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment amount = VAL_cw_contract_support_refund producer = VAL } }
			log = "[VAL_CONTRACT] contract cancelled: incomplete equipment payment refunded"
		}
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_arms_offer_pending
	country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_cw.51 }
}

# COUNTRY; FROM is the paid state. The country receipt blocks all other targets.
VAL_reclamation_begin_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_reclamation_deposit }
			ADISCORD_economy_can_spend_500 = yes
			VAL_reclamation_project_target_valid = yes
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
		set_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 }
		FROM = { set_state_flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress }
	}
}

# COUNTRY: resolve the paid state from its receipt; tooltip callbacks may lack a state FROM.
VAL_reclamation_refund_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } }
		24 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		42 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		48 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		54 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		55 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		56 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
		57 = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
	}
}

# STATE: the state flag binds the country receipt to exactly one paid project.
VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress
			VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } }
		}
		clr_state_flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress
		VAL = {
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAL_reclamation_deposit }
			add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = VAL_reclamation_deposit }
			clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_deposit
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY: resolve the paid state from its receipt; tooltip callbacks may lack a state FROM.
VAL_reclamation_finish_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } }
		24 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		42 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		48 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		54 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		55 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		56 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
		57 = { VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes }
	}
}

VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress
			VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { VAL_reclamation_state_project_valid = yes }
			VAL = { clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_deposit }
			clr_state_flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } }
				if = {
					limit = { infrastructure < 5 }
					add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
				}
				else = { add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1 }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 1 compare = equals } }
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
			}
			else = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				if = { limit = { industrial_complex < 20 } add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 1 instant_build = yes } }
			}
			add_to_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 1 }
			VAL_reclamation_refresh_state = yes
			VAL = { VAL_complete_reclamation = yes ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		}
		else = { VAL_reclamation_refund_state_project = yes }
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL: the idea itself is the receipt; preserve the separate focus reward flag.
VAL_complete_reclamation = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_idea = VAL_harvest_of_ash VAL_reclamation_complete = yes }
		remove_ideas = VAL_harvest_of_ash
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# COUNTRY: only an outstanding receipt opens the fixed seven-state recovery pool.
VAL_reclamation_reconcile_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } }
		24 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		42 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		48 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		54 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		55 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		56 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
		57 = { VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = yes }
	}
}

# STATE: never restart a paid deadline or let a missing native timer hold escrow forever.
VAL_reclamation_reconcile_state_project = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = { flag = VAL_reclamation_work_in_progress days > 89 } }
		VAL_reclamation_finish_state_project = yes
	}
}

# STATE: keep one restoration status through ownership changes; untreated states stay contaminated.
VAL_reclamation_refresh_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_recovered_land } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_recovered_land }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = ADISCORD_vorkerland_dirty_state }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_1_modifier } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_1_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_2_modifier } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_2_modifier }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_3_modifier } }
			remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_3_modifier }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_3_modifier }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 2 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_2_modifier }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 1 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
			add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_reclamation_stage_1_modifier }
		}

		# Remove variables used by the retired compensation modifier.
		clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_people
		clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_resources
		clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_slots
		clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_construction
		clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_supply
	}
}

# State receipts keep paid industrial projects independent across regions.
VAL_reclamation_begin_industry = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			VAL_reclamation_industry_target_valid = yes
			check_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			FROM = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit } }
		}
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
		FROM = { set_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit value = 1000 } }
	}
}

VAL_reclamation_refund_industry = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit value = 1000 compare = equals } } }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1000 }
		FROM = { clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_reclamation_finish_industry = {
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit value = 1000 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { VAL_reclamation_industry_target_valid = yes }
			FROM = {
				clear_variable = VAL_reclamation_industry_deposit
				set_variable = { var = VAL_reclamation_stage value = 4 }
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
				add_building_construction = { type = industrial_complex level = 2 instant_build = yes }
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else = { VAL_reclamation_refund_industry = yes }
	}
}

# Foreign campaign: 1 = ultimatum, 2 = refusal, 3 = war, 0 = closed.
# Target codes: 1 CIN, 2 OSF, 3 APH, 4 ERT. Only one campaign may be open.
VAL_frontier_close = {
    remove_mission = VAL_nod_ultimatum
    clr_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_issued
    clr_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired
    set_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 }
    CIN = { clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated }
    OSF = { clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated }
    APH = { clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated }
    ERT = { clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated }
    remove_mission = VAL_frontier_ultimatum_deadline
    remove_ideas = VAL_frontier_transport_columns
    clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_armistice_offered
    # Keep campaign majors until all campaign war relations have ended.
    NOD = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
    STP = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } has_war_with = CIN } white_peace = CIN }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } has_war_with = OSF } white_peace = OSF }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } has_war_with = APH } white_peace = APH }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = equals } has_war_with = ERT } white_peace = ERT }
    CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
    OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
    APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    NOD = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    }
    STP = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    }
    CIN = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    }
    OSF = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    }
    APH = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
    }
    VAL_frontier_release_coalition = yes
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# The saved leader identifies the existing alliance; only new memberships carry receipts.
VAL_frontier_join_coalition = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_event_target = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
        if = {
            limit = {
                is_in_faction = no
                event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor = { exists = yes is_in_faction = yes }
                OR = { is_subject = no overlord = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor } }
            }
            save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_joining_country
            event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor = { faction_leader = { add_to_faction = event_target:VAL_frontier_joining_country } }
            if = {
                limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
                set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_to_coalition
            }
        }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_to_coalition }
        if = {
            limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor is_faction_leader = no }
            faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = PREV }
        }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_to_coalition
    }
}

VAL_frontier_release_coalition = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_event_target = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
        event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_created_coalition
                    is_in_faction = yes
                    NOT = {
                        any_other_country = {
                            is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor
                            NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_to_coalition }
                        }
                    }
                }
                faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
            }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_created_coalition
        }
        CIN = { VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = yes }
        OSF = { VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = yes }
        APH = { VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = yes }
        NOD = { VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = yes }
        STP = { VAL_frontier_leave_coalition = yes }
        clear_global_event_target = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor
    }
}

# Target scope: the tribes keep their own alliance; guarantors enter only after the deadline.
VAL_frontier_assemble_coalition = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
        faction_leader = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
    }
    else = {
        create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = VAL_frontier_defence_coalition }
        set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_created_coalition
        save_global_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor
    }
    CIN = { VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes }
    OSF = { VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes }
    APH = { VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes }
}

VAL_frontier_prepare_major = {
    if = { limit = { is_major = no } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major set_major = yes }
}

VAL_frontier_record_ultimatum = {
    set_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 1 }
    VAL_frontier_save_quoted_target = yes
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = less_than } }
        event_target:VAL_frontier_quoted_target = { VAL_frontier_assemble_coalition = yes }
    }
    activate_mission = VAL_frontier_ultimatum_deadline
}

VAL_frontier_refuse = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_offer_is_current = yes }
        VAL = {
            VAL_frontier_show_refusal = yes
        }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_accept = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_offer_is_current = yes }
        VAL = { VAL_frontier_settle_victory = yes }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_settle_victory = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
        set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_treaty_signed
        set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_CIN value = 0 }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } CIN = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } }
            set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_CIN value = 1 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_OSF value = 0 }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } OSF = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } }
            set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_OSF value = 1 }
        }
        set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_APH value = 0 }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } APH = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } }
            set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_APH value = 1 }
        }
        VAL_frontier_close = yes
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_CIN value = 1 compare = equals } } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_frontier_commissioners CIN = { exists = yes VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = VAL } } 58 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } 59 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } 60 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } }
                set_autonomy = { target = CIN autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
                CIN = {
                    set_cosmetic_tag = CIN_VAL_commissariat
                    set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
                    promote_character = { character = CIN_VAL_commission ideology = contractual_etatism }
                    set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
                    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
                }
            }
            else = {
                if = { limit = { 58 = { is_owned_by = CIN OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } } transfer_state = 58 }
                if = { limit = { 59 = { is_owned_by = CIN OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } } transfer_state = 59 }
                if = { limit = { 60 = { is_owned_by = CIN OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = CIN } } } transfer_state = 60 }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_OSF value = 1 compare = equals } } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_frontier_commissioners OSF = { exists = yes VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = VAL } } 61 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } 62 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } 63 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } }
                set_autonomy = { target = OSF autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
                OSF = {
                    set_cosmetic_tag = OSF_VAL_commissariat
                    set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
                    promote_character = { character = OSF_VAL_commission ideology = contractual_etatism }
                    set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
                    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
                }
            }
            else = {
                if = { limit = { 61 = { is_owned_by = OSF OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } } transfer_state = 61 }
                if = { limit = { 62 = { is_owned_by = OSF OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } } transfer_state = 62 }
                if = { limit = { 63 = { is_owned_by = OSF OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = OSF } } } transfer_state = 63 }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_settle_APH value = 1 compare = equals } } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_completed_focus = VAL_frontier_commissioners APH = { exists = yes VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = VAL } } 64 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = APH } } 65 = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = APH } } }
                set_autonomy = { target = APH autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
                APH = {
                    set_cosmetic_tag = APH_VAL_commissariat
                    set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
                    promote_character = { character = APH_VAL_commission ideology = contractual_etatism }
                    set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
                    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
                }
            }
            else = {
                if = { limit = { 64 = { is_owned_by = APH OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = APH } } } transfer_state = 64 }
                if = { limit = { 65 = { is_owned_by = APH OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = APH } } } transfer_state = 65 }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = equals } }
                if = { limit = { 168 = { is_owned_by = ERT OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = ERT } } } transfer_state = 168 }
        }
        VAL_form_northern_administration = yes
        add_stability = 0.05
        country_event = { id = val_rework.115 }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_settle_armistice = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
        set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_armistice_signed
        set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_party_receives_border value = 0 }
        if = {
            limit = { STP = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_war_with = VAL has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle } }
            set_temp_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_party_receives_border value = 1 }
        }
        VAL_frontier_close = yes
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_party_receives_border value = 1 compare = equals } STP = { exists = yes has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle } }
            if = { limit = { 42 = { is_owned_by = VAL OR = { is_controlled_by = VAL is_controlled_by = STP is_controlled_by = NOD } } } STP = { transfer_state = 42 } }
            if = { limit = { 55 = { is_owned_by = VAL OR = { is_controlled_by = VAL is_controlled_by = STP is_controlled_by = NOD } } } STP = { transfer_state = 55 } }
        }
        add_stability = -0.10
        country_event = { id = val_rework.116 }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_settle_defeat = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
        # Peace clears guarantor membership and subordination can change the
        # subject hierarchy. Preserve only this losing side's Stelander cores.
        clear_temp_array = VAL_frontier_returned_cores
        clear_temp_array = VAL_frontier_core_donors
        if = {
            limit = { STP = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_war_with = VAL has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle } }
            add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_frontier_core_donors value = THIS }
            every_owned_state = {
                limit = { is_core_of = STP }
                add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_frontier_returned_cores value = THIS }
            }
            every_subject_country = {
                add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_frontier_core_donors value = THIS }
                every_owned_state = {
                    limit = { is_core_of = STP }
                    add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_frontier_returned_cores value = THIS }
                }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOD = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_capitulated = no } capital_scope = { controller = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor } } }
            VAL_frontier_settle_armistice = yes
            NOD = { puppet = { target = VAL end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no } }
            set_cosmetic_tag = VAL_dopotroydsk
            set_politics = { ruling_party = pragmatism elections_allowed = no }
            promote_character = { character = VAL_party_administration ideology = pragmatism_ideology }
            set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        else = { VAL_frontier_settle_armistice = yes }
        for_each_scope_loop = {
            array = VAL_frontier_returned_cores
            STP = { transfer_state = PREV ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
        }
        for_each_scope_loop = {
            array = VAL_frontier_core_donors
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        clear_temp_array = VAL_frontier_returned_cores
        clear_temp_array = VAL_frontier_core_donors
    }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. All enrolled cannibal states receive their own declaration in the
# same execution. Do not depend on allied AI accepting an add_to_war call.
VAL_frontier_declare_all_tribal_members = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            CIN = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member }
            NOT = { has_war_with = CIN }
        }
        declare_war_on = { target = CIN type = take_state_focus generator = { 58 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            OSF = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member }
            NOT = { has_war_with = OSF }
        }
        declare_war_on = { target = OSF type = take_state_focus generator = { 61 } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = {
            APH = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member }
            NOT = { has_war_with = APH }
        }
        declare_war_on = { target = APH type = take_state_focus generator = { 64 } }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_start_war = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 2 compare = equals } VAL_frontier_prewar_eligible = yes has_war = no }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 }
        VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } }
            CIN = { VAL_frontier_assemble_coalition = yes }
            CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            VAL_frontier_declare_all_tribal_members = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } }
            OSF = { VAL_frontier_assemble_coalition = yes }
            CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            VAL_frontier_declare_all_tribal_members = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } }
            APH = { VAL_frontier_assemble_coalition = yes }
            CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
            VAL_frontier_declare_all_tribal_members = yes
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = equals } }
            declare_war_on = { target = ERT type = take_state_focus generator = { 168 } }
        }
        VAL_frontier_join_existing_war = yes
        # A declaration may enqueue its native relation. Confirm once after that tick,
        # retaining the quoted target so an old callback cannot close a later campaign.
        VAL_frontier_save_quoted_target = yes
        hidden_effect = { country_event = { id = val_rework.117 hours = 1 } }
    }
    else = { VAL_frontier_close = yes }
}

# COUNTRY VAL: invite participants only into the selected target's live war.
VAL_frontier_join_existing_war = {
    VAL_frontier_issue_nod_ultimatum = yes
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } has_war_with = CIN }
        CIN = { VAL_frontier_call_members = yes }
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired }
            NOD = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = STP } VAL = { VAL_stelander_dominated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = CIN has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = CIN enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
            STP = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle NOD = { has_war_with = VAL } OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = NOD } is_in_faction_with = NOD NOT = { has_war_with = CIN has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = CIN enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } has_war_with = OSF }
        OSF = { VAL_frontier_call_members = yes }
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired }
            NOD = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = STP } VAL = { VAL_stelander_dominated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = OSF has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = OSF enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
            STP = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle NOD = { has_war_with = VAL } OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = NOD } is_in_faction_with = NOD NOT = { has_war_with = OSF has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = OSF enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } has_war_with = APH }
        APH = { VAL_frontier_call_members = yes }
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired }
            NOD = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = STP } VAL = { VAL_stelander_dominated = yes } NOT = { has_war_with = APH has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = APH enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
            STP = {
                if = {
                    limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle NOD = { has_war_with = VAL } OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = NOD } is_in_faction_with = NOD NOT = { has_war_with = APH has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = APH enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                    if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

# COUNTRY: native war-entry reconciliation.

VAL_frontier_register_war_participant = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_war_with = VAL
            VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
            OR = {
                AND = { VAL = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired } OR = { tag = NOD AND = { tag = STP is_in_faction_with = NOD NOD = { has_war_with = VAL } } } }
                AND = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } } OR = { tag = CIN is_in_faction_with = CIN } }
                AND = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } } OR = { tag = OSF is_in_faction_with = OSF } }
                AND = { VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } } OR = { tag = APH is_in_faction_with = APH } }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { tag = CIN tag = OSF tag = APH } }
            set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                OR = {
                    AND = { tag = NOD OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = STP } }
                    AND = { tag = STP has_country_flag = STP_cw_won_union_battle OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = NOD } }
                }
            }
            set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor
            VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
        }
    }
}


VAL_frontier_reconcile_active_members = {
    # Repair missing entries before checking victory, using only the active campaign target.
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } has_war_with = CIN } CIN = { VAL_frontier_reconcile_members = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } has_war_with = OSF } OSF = { VAL_frontier_reconcile_members = yes } }
        if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } has_war_with = APH } APH = { VAL_frontier_reconcile_members = yes } }
    }
}

# Surrender progress and prewar eligibility retain a bounded periodic check.
VAL_frontier_weekly = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } VAL_frontier_has_campaign_war = yes NOT = { AND = { VAL_frontier_target_occupied = yes VAL_frontier_members_beaten = yes } } surrender_progress > 0.65 NOD = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor has_war_with = VAL has_capitulated = no } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_armistice_offered } }
        set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_armistice_offered
        VAL_frontier_save_quoted_target = yes
        NOD = { country_event = { id = val_rework.113 } }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = less_than } VAL_frontier_prewar_eligible = no }
        VAL_frontier_close = yes
    }
}

# Native notifications may run before ownership and war relations finish changing.
VAL_queue_frontier_reconciliation = {
    VAL = {
        if = {
            limit = { exists = yes check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
            country_event = { id = val_rework.102 hours = 1 }
        }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_reconcile = {
    VAL_frontier_reconcile_active_members = yes
    CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated capital_scope = { NOT = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated } }
    OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated capital_scope = { NOT = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated } }
    APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated capital_scope = { NOT = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated } }
    ERT = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated capital_scope = { NOT = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated } }

    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
        # Victory must be evaluated before generic "the war relation is gone" cleanup.
        # Native peace/conference callbacks can arrive only after has_war_with has
        # already become false, while the occupied states and defeat receipts still
        # prove the authored campaign result.
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_frontier_target_occupied = yes VAL_frontier_members_beaten = yes }
            VAL_frontier_settle_victory = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { OR = { VAL_frontier_has_campaign_war = no AND = { VAL_frontier_target_owned = no VAL_frontier_target_occupied = no } } }
            VAL_frontier_close = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = {
                check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = equals }
                ERT = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_defeated capital_scope = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes } }
                168 = { NOT = { OR = { VAL_frontier_controlled = yes is_controlled_by = ERT } } }
            }
            # The separate occupier retains its result; no treaty reward was delivered.
            VAL_frontier_close = yes
        }
        else_if = { limit = { has_war = no } VAL_frontier_close = yes }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than } OR = { has_capitulated = yes VAL_frontier_prewar_eligible = no } }
        VAL_frontier_close = yes
    }
}

# The event keeps this local address even if a later ultimatum changes country variables.
VAL_frontier_save_quoted_target = {
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 1 compare = equals } } CIN = { save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_quoted_target } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 2 compare = equals } } OSF = { save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_quoted_target } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 3 compare = equals } } APH = { save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_quoted_target } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = equals } } ERT = { save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_quoted_target } }
}

VAL_frontier_show_refusal = {
    set_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 2 }
    remove_mission = VAL_frontier_ultimatum_deadline
    VAL_frontier_save_quoted_target = yes
    country_event = { id = val_rework.111 }
}

# COUNTRY CIN/OSF/APH. Membership is the receipt for this campaign's peace and majors.
VAL_frontier_enrol_member = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_member_eligible = yes }
        VAL_frontier_join_coalition = yes
        if = {
            limit = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
            set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            ADISCORD_release_non_participating_minor_optimization = yes
        }
    }
}

# Target country scope. Reconcile the three tribes without replacing campaign receipts.
VAL_frontier_reconcile_members = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_war_with = VAL
            is_in_faction = yes
        }
        faction_leader = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
        CIN = {
            if = {
                limit = { has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
                set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            }
            if = {
                limit = { VAL_frontier_member_eligible = yes }
                VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes
            }
        }
        OSF = {
            if = {
                limit = { has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
                set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            }
            if = {
                limit = { VAL_frontier_member_eligible = yes }
                VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes
            }
        }
        APH = {
            if = {
                limit = { has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_coalition_anchor }
                set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            }
            if = {
                limit = { VAL_frontier_member_eligible = yes }
                VAL_frontier_enrol_member = yes
            }
        }
        VAL_frontier_call_members = yes
        CIN = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
        OSF = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
        APH = { if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member has_war_with = VAL } VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes } }
    }
}

# Target scope: all enrolled tribes enter the target's existing war, never a parallel war.
VAL_frontier_call_members = {
    save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_war_target
    CIN = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member is_subject = no has_capitulated = no is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
            add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
        }
    }
    OSF = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member is_subject = no has_capitulated = no is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
            add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
        }
    }
    APH = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member is_subject = no has_capitulated = no is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            VAL_frontier_prepare_major = yes
            add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = event_target:VAL_frontier_war_target enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_member
            if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_added_major }
        }
    }
}

# Промышленный цикл. Ступень кризиса независима от довоенных вложений;
# выпуск, строительство и бюджет пересчитываются только при изменении входов.
VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_output value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_construction value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_efficiency value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_trade value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_income value = 0 }
	set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_consumer value = 0 }
	# Recovery progress is the durable crisis marker. If the local presentation flag
	# is ever lost between callbacks, restore it before recalculating the consumer.
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted }
			has_variable = VAL_economic_recovery_steps
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted
				has_variable = VAL_economic_recovery_steps
				has_country_flag = VAL_economic_recovery_completed
			}
		}
		clamp_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps min = 0 max = 9 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_output value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_output value = 0.09 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_output value = -0.75 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_construction value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_construction value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_construction value = -0.57 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_efficiency value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_efficiency value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_efficiency value = -0.57 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_trade value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_trade value = 0.10 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_trade value = -0.85 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_income value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_income value = 0.07 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_income value = -0.57 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_consumer value = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
		multiply_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_consumer value = -0.02 }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_consumer value = 0.18 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_arsenal_investment } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_output value = VAL_arsenal_investment } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = VAL_civilian_investment } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_industrial_construction value = VAL_civilian_investment } }
	remove_ideas = { VAL_vorkerland_contract_disruptions VAL_worldwide_famous_weponry }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted }
			NOT = { has_variable = VAL_economic_recovery_steps }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_economic_recovery_completed }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_contract_industry } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_industry } }
	}
	else_if = {
		# The collapse persists until the final recovery focus explicitly commits
		# completion. A numeric stage by itself must never make the crisis vanish.
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_economic_recovery_completed }
				check_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps value = 9 compare = less_than }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_contract_industry } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_industry } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } }
	}
	else = {
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_contract_industry } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_contract_industry } }
		if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_collapse } }
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_economic_miracle } }
	}
	force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

VAL_advance_economic_recovery = {
	if = {
		limit = { hidden_trigger = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted check_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps value = 9 compare = less_than } } }
		effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = VAL_economic_recovery_delta }
		if = {
			limit = { hidden_trigger = { check_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps value = 8 compare = equals } } }
			custom_effect_tooltip = VAL_economic_miracle_unlock_tt
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_variable = { var = VAL_economic_recovery_steps value = 1 }
			VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = yes
		}
	}
}
VAL_invest_arsenal_industry = {
	effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = VAL_arsenal_investment_delta }
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_investment value = 0.02 }
		VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = yes
	}
}
VAL_invest_civilian_industry = {
	effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = VAL_civilian_investment_delta }
	hidden_effect = {
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_civilian_investment value = 0.03 }
		VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = yes
	}
}




# --- livonn_settlement_effects ---
# Kefreyt's postwar handling of Livonn.
# VAL first occupies the district as part of the military settlement. If the
# prewar Shabrat agreement survives, the player then chooses whether to honor it.

VAL_cw_stage_livonn_settlement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_postwar
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_staged }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_resolved }
		}

		# Formation is the authoritative territorial step. Settlement and control
		# callbacks stage the political choice only after Livonn is actually in OCA.
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { 45 = { is_owned_by = OCA is_controlled_by = OCA } } }
			VAL_form_occidian_administration = yes
		}

		# Do not resolve Livonn before the Stelander civil-war winner is known.
		# STP_cw_check_union_wars_finished calls this effect again when the flag is set.
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_global_flag = STP_cw_union_wars_finished
				45 = { is_owned_by = OCA is_controlled_by = OCA }
			}
			set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_staged
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						AND = {
							45 = { has_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset }
							STS = { STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes }
						}
						STP = {
							STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
							has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
						}
					}
				}
				set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
			}
			else = {
				set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_resolved
				set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_retained
			}
		}
	}
}

VAL_cw_honor_livonn_agreement = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
			45 = { is_owned_by = OCA is_controlled_by = OCA }
			OCA = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					45 = { has_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset }
					STS = { STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes }
				}
				STP = {
					STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
					has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				45 = { has_state_flag = STP_local_deal_asset }
				STS = { STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes }
			}
			STS = { transfer_state = 45 }
			45 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = STS }
			set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_agreement_honored
			clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_returned_to_party
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				STP = {
					STP_pw_can_reconstruct = yes
					has_country_flag = STP_sided_with_the_party_flag
				}
			}
			STP = { transfer_state = 45 }
			45 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = STP }
			set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_returned_to_party
			clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_agreement_honored
		}

		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_resolved
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_retained
		OCA = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		VAL_reconcile_occidian_resource_rights = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_cw_retain_livonn = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			has_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
			OCA = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL }
			45 = { is_owned_by = OCA }
		}
		45 = { set_state_controller_to = OCA }
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_resolved
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_retained
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_agreement_honored
	}
}


# COUNTRY VAL, target in FROM. Each state owns its receipt, allowing parallel local work.
VAL_frontier_begin_workshop = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_workshop_target_valid = yes ADISCORD_economy_can_spend_500 = yes FROM = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit } } }
        ADISCORD_economy_spend_500 = yes
        FROM = { set_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit value = 500 } }
    }
}
VAL_frontier_refund_workshop = {
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } } }
        FROM = { clear_variable = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
}
VAL_frontier_finish_workshop = {
    if = {
        limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit value = 500 compare = equals } } }
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_frontier_workshop_target_valid = yes }
            FROM = {
                clear_variable = VAL_frontier_workshop_deposit
                add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
                add_building_construction = { type = arms_factory level = 1 instant_build = yes }
                owner = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
            }
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        else = { VAL_frontier_refund_workshop = yes }
    }
}

VAL_recall_northern_volunteers = {
 if = { limit = { YPR = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } } } recall_volunteers_from = YPR }
 if = { limit = { COF = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } } } recall_volunteers_from = COF }
 if = { limit = { TFF = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } } } recall_volunteers_from = TFF }
 if = { limit = { STS = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } } } recall_volunteers_from = STS }
 if = { limit = { STP = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } } } recall_volunteers_from = STP }
}

VAL_reconcile_northern_volunteers = {
 if = { limit = { YPR = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } NOT = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } } } recall_volunteers_from = YPR }
 if = { limit = { COF = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } NOT = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } } } recall_volunteers_from = COF }
 if = { limit = { TFF = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } NOT = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } } } recall_volunteers_from = TFF }
 if = { limit = { STS = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } NOT = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } } } recall_volunteers_from = STS }
 if = { limit = { STP = { has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 0 } NOT = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } } } recall_volunteers_from = STP }
}

# COUNTRY VAL: refresh the shortage after an authored supply change.
VAL_reconcile_supply_crisis = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted }
		VAL_assert_vorkerland_metal_shortage = yes
	}
}
# COUNTRY VAL: first-defeat transition; the council has its own later war route.
# This closes every inherited offensive pipeline before the technical peace
# and replaces Solgalov's campaign tree with the commanders' recovery program.
VAL_enter_stelander_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_defeated }
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_stelander_defeated
		if = { limit = { has_power_balance = { id = VAL_command_balance } } remove_power_balance = { id = VAL_command_balance } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_defeated
		set_country_flag = VAL_cw_settled
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_postwar
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_entered
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_mobilizing
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_military_course
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_trade_course
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_offer_pending
		clr_country_flag = VAL_campaign_mobilizing
		clr_country_flag = VAL_stelander_offer_pending
		clr_country_flag = VAL_foreign_operation_active
		clr_country_flag = VAL_cw_livonn_settlement_pending
		remove_mission = VAL_cw_intervention_preparation
		SRP = { remove_mission = STP_cw_val_ultimatum }
		STP = {
			clr_country_flag = VAL_campaign_target
			clr_country_flag = VAL_stelander_offer
		}
		STS = {
			clr_country_flag = VAL_campaign_target
			clr_country_flag = VAL_stelander_offer
		}
		NOD = { clr_country_flag = VAL_campaign_target }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			VAL_frontier_close = yes
		}
		VAL_clear_foreign_operation = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov }
			retire_character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Commanders_Council }
			promote_character = { character = VAL_Commanders_Council ideology = contractual_etatism }
		}
		set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
		load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_defeated_focus keep_completed = no }
		mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		log = "[VAL] Shabrat defeat transition: Solgalov removed, commanders council installed"
	}
}

# COUNTRY STS after its military defeat by VAL; the existing peace dispatcher
# has already ended their pair and keeps unrelated wars separate.
VAL_enforce_stelander_defeat = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = STS check_variable = { var = STP_cw_capitulation_occupier value = 5 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_opponent value = 1 compare = equals } check_variable = { var = STP_pc_this_result value = 2 compare = equals } VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no } }
  VAL_install_stelander_administration = yes
 }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. The resource belt stays under direct rule; five states form NKA.
# Existing subjects with foreign land keep that land and their separate government.
VAL_form_northern_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_can_form_northern_administration = yes }
        # Seed from a country that survives the transfer, preserving its army
        # until the following bounded annexation can move it to the administration.
        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = no } 58 = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } } } }
            NKA = { transfer_state = 58 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = no } 62 = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } } } }
            NKA = { transfer_state = 62 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = no } 63 = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } } } }
            NKA = { transfer_state = 63 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = no } 64 = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } } } }
            NKA = { transfer_state = 64 }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = no } 65 = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } } } }
            NKA = { transfer_state = 65 }
        }

        if = {
            limit = { NKA = { exists = yes } }
            if = {
                limit = { CIN = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes } }
                if = { limit = { CIN = { is_ai = no } } NKA = { change_tag_from = CIN } }
                NKA = { annex_country = { target = CIN transfer_troops = yes } }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { OSF = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes } }
                if = { limit = { OSF = { is_ai = no } } NKA = { change_tag_from = OSF } }
                NKA = { annex_country = { target = OSF transfer_troops = yes } }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { APH = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL VAL_frontier_has_only_claimed_states = yes } }
                if = { limit = { APH = { is_ai = no } } NKA = { change_tag_from = APH } }
                NKA = { annex_country = { target = APH transfer_troops = yes } }
            }
            58 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    NKA = { transfer_state = 58 }
                }
                add_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = NKA
            }
            59 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NKA owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    VAL = { transfer_state = 59 }
                }
                remove_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = VAL
            }
            60 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NKA owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    VAL = { transfer_state = 60 }
                }
                remove_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = VAL
            }
            61 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NKA owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    VAL = { transfer_state = 61 }
                }
                remove_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = VAL
            }
            62 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    NKA = { transfer_state = 62 }
                }
                add_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = NKA
            }
            63 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    NKA = { transfer_state = 63 }
                }
                add_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = NKA
            }
            64 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    NKA = { transfer_state = 64 }
                }
                add_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = NKA
            }
            65 = {
                if = {
                    limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = VAL owner = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
                    NKA = { transfer_state = 65 }
                }
                add_core_of = NKA
                set_state_controller_to = NKA
            }
            NKA = {
                set_capital = { state = 64 remember_old_capital = no }
                if = {
                    limit = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VAL } }
                    inherit_technology = VAL
                    set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
                    promote_character = { character = NKA_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism }
                }
                set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
                ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
                ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
            }
            set_autonomy = { target = NKA autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
            country_event = { id = val_contract.346 }
        }
    }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. Create the administration before absorbing a defeated country,
# so its army and a player can follow the bounded military settlement.
VAL_form_occidian_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_can_form_occidian_administration = yes }

		# Seed OCA with a state whose donor survives the first transfer. During the
		# military settlement republican Livonn can provide a fallback; districts
		# belonging to STP/STS or another country are never eligible.
		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { exists = no }
				43 = { owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } }
			}
			OCA = { transfer_state = 43 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { exists = no }
				44 = { owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } }
			}
			OCA = { transfer_state = 44 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { exists = no }
				45 = { VAL_livonn_available_for_administration = yes owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } }
			}
			OCA = { transfer_state = 45 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { exists = no }
				88 = { owner = { check_variable = { var = num_owned_states value = 1 compare = greater_than } } }
			}
			OCA = { transfer_state = 88 }
		}

		if = {
			limit = { OCA = { exists = yes } }

			if = {
				limit = {
					SRP = { exists = yes has_war = no VAL_only_occidian_states = yes }
					OR = {
						SRP = { is_subject_of = VAL }
						AND = {
							has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_settlement_pending
							SRP = { is_subject = no }
						}
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = { SRP = { is_ai = no } }
					OCA = { change_tag_from = SRP }
				}
				OCA = { annex_country = { target = SRP transfer_troops = yes } }
			}

			OCA = {
				transfer_state = 43
				transfer_state = 44
				if = { limit = { 45 = { VAL_livonn_available_for_administration = yes } } transfer_state = 45 }
				transfer_state = 88
				set_capital = { state = 43 remember_old_capital = no }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VAL } }
					inherit_technology = VAL
					set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
