					promote_character = { character = OCA_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism }
				}
				set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
				ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
				ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			}

			43 = { add_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = OCA }
			44 = { add_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = OCA }
			45 = { if = { limit = { is_owned_by = OCA } add_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = OCA } }
			88 = { add_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = OCA }

			if = {
				limit = { OCA = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VAL } } }
				set_autonomy = {
					target = OCA
					autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration
					freedom_level = 0.10
					end_wars = no
					end_civil_wars = no
				}
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
			}
			OCA = { VAL_initialize_balchansk_administration = yes }
			VAL_reconcile_occidian_resource_rights = yes
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_settlement_pending
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL. A subordinate Occidian Contract Administration immediately grants
# Kefreyt resource rights over the districts it actually owns. Per-state flags
# make repeated reconciliation idempotent and allow Livonin to leave the
# contract cleanly if the prewar Shabrat settlement is honoured.
VAL_reconcile_occidian_resource_rights = {
	if = {
		limit = { OCA = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL } }

		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { owns_state = 43 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43 }
			}
			OCA = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 43 } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43 OCA = { NOT = { owns_state = 43 } } }
			remove_resource_rights = 43
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { owns_state = 44 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44 }
			}
			OCA = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 44 } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44 OCA = { NOT = { owns_state = 44 } } }
			remove_resource_rights = 44
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { owns_state = 45 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45 }
			}
			OCA = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 45 } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45 OCA = { NOT = { owns_state = 45 } } }
			remove_resource_rights = 45
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { owns_state = 88 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88 }
			}
			OCA = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 88 } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88 OCA = { NOT = { owns_state = 88 } } }
			remove_resource_rights = 88
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88
		}

		if = {
			limit = {
				OCA = { owns_state = 46 }
				NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46 }
			}
			OCA = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 46 } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46 OCA = { NOT = { owns_state = 46 } } }
			remove_resource_rights = 46
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46
		}
	}
	else = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43 }
			remove_resource_rights = 43
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_43
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44 }
			remove_resource_rights = 44
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_44
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45 }
			remove_resource_rights = 45
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_45
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88 }
			remove_resource_rights = 88
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_88
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46 }
			remove_resource_rights = 46
			clr_country_flag = VAL_occidian_resource_rights_46
		}
	}
}


VAL_integrate_occidia = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_occidian_administration_secured = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { OCA = { VAL_only_occidian_states = yes } }
			if = {
				limit = { is_ai = yes OCA = { is_ai = no } }
				change_tag_from = OCA
			}
			annex_country = { target = OCA transfer_troops = yes }
		}
		else = {
			transfer_state = 43
			transfer_state = 44
			if = {
				limit = { 45 = { is_owned_by = OCA } }
				transfer_state = 45
			}
			if = {
				limit = { 46 = { is_owned_by = OCA } }
				transfer_state = 46
			}
			transfer_state = 88
			OCA = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		}
		43 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = VAL }
		44 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = VAL }
		if = {
			limit = { 45 = { is_owned_by = VAL } }
			45 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = VAL }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { 46 = { is_owned_by = VAL } }
			46 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = VAL }
		}
		88 = { remove_core_of = OCA set_state_controller_to = VAL }
		VAL_reconcile_occidian_resource_rights = yes
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# --- command_balance ---
VAL_initialize_command_balance = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_command_balance_initialized } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_defeated } }
		set_power_balance = { id = VAL_command_balance set_value = 0.10 }
		set_country_flag = VAL_command_balance_initialized
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL. When Shabrat and Kefreyt defeat NOD in the same war, neither
# side receives the whole country by callback order. NOD-owned states follow
# their actual controller at surrender; northern states already settled to YPR
# are therefore left untouched.
VAL_begin_joint_nod_shabrat_campaign = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_shabrat_partition_completed }
			STS = {
				exists = yes
				has_capitulated = no
				is_subject = no
				has_war_with = NOD
				has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes }
			}
			NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = STS }
			OR = {
				is_in_faction_with = STS
				AND = {
					is_in_faction = no
					STS = { is_in_faction = no }
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts
		NOD = { set_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_defeat_pending }
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = STS } }
			create_faction_from_template = {
				template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard
				name = VAL_stelander_equal_alliance_name
			}
			add_to_faction = STS
			set_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_created_faction
		}
		add_to_war = {
			targeted_alliance = STS
			enemy = NOD
			hostility_reason = asked_to_join
			single_target_only = yes
		}
		VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes
	}
}

VAL_release_joint_nod_shabrat_alliance = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_created_faction
			is_in_faction = yes
			is_faction_leader = yes
			STS = { exists = yes is_in_faction_with = VAL }
			NOT = {
				any_other_country = {
					is_in_faction_with = VAL
					NOT = { tag = STS }
				}
			}
		}
		dismantle_faction = yes
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_created_faction
}

VAL_snapshot_joint_nodrul_settlement = {
	clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_ECA_states
	clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_YPR_states
	clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_DCA_states
	if = {
		limit = { ECA = { exists = no } }
		17 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
		18 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { ECA = { is_subject_of = VAL } }
		17 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } ECA = { transfer_state = 17 } add_core_of = ECA set_state_controller_to = ECA } }
		18 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } ECA = { transfer_state = 18 } add_core_of = ECA set_state_controller_to = ECA } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { YPR = { OR = { exists = no is_subject_of = NOD } } DCA = { exists = no } }
		19 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
		20 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
		21 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
		22 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
		8 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
		15 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
		16 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
	}
}

VAL_settle_joint_nod_shabrat_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_shabrat_partition_completed }
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			STS = {
				exists = yes
				has_capitulated = no
				has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes }
			}
			NOD = {
				exists = yes
				OR = {
					has_capitulated = yes
					has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_defeat_pending
				}
			}
		}

		# The joint bargain is territorial rather than occupation-based.
		# Shabrat receives the southern Nodrul core; Kefreyt administers the north.
		10 = {
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NOD owner = { is_subject_of = NOD } } }
				STS = { transfer_state = 10 }
				add_core_of = STS
				set_state_controller_to = STS
			}
		}
		11 = {
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NOD owner = { is_subject_of = NOD } } }
				STS = { transfer_state = 11 }
				add_core_of = STS
				set_state_controller_to = STS
			}
		}
		12 = {
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NOD owner = { is_subject_of = NOD } } }
				STS = { transfer_state = 12 }
				add_core_of = STS
				set_state_controller_to = STS
			}
		}
		30 = {
			if = {
				limit = { OR = { is_owned_by = NOD owner = { is_subject_of = NOD } } }
				STS = { transfer_state = 30 }
				add_core_of = STS
				set_state_controller_to = STS
			}
		}

		VAL_snapshot_joint_nodrul_settlement = yes
		NOD = {
			set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_joint_nod_settlement_consumed days = 2 }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = STS } white_peace = STS }
		}
		VAL_partition_nodrul_settlement = yes
		NOD = {
			if = { limit = { owns_state = 13 } set_capital = 13 }
			VAL_prepare_nodrul_administration = yes
			clr_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_defeat_pending
			clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
			clr_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate
		}
		STS = {
			NOD = { set_country_flag = STP_pc_defeated_by_sts }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { var = STP_pc_course value = 2 compare = equals } }
				set_country_flag = STP_pc_lib_nod_won
			}
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_shabrat_partition_completed
		VAL_release_joint_nod_shabrat_alliance = yes
		clr_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		NOD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

# --- final_campaign_administrations ---
# COUNTRY STP/STS. Reuse the surviving country so armies, technology and the
# player's seat remain intact. Only owned, uncontested border states are ceded.
VAL_install_stelander_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS } VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no } }
        save_event_target_as = VAL_settlement_country
        VAL_settle_frontier_partner_war = yes
        STP_close_competing_ultimatum_wars_after_defeat = yes
        if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { NOT = { is_subject_of = VAL } NOT = { has_autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration } } }
            VAL = {
                set_autonomy = { target = event_target:VAL_settlement_country autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
                VAL = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
            }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_cosmetic_tag = STL_VAL_administration } }
            set_cosmetic_tag = STL_VAL_administration
            set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
            if = {
                limit = { tag = STP }
                if = { limit = { has_character = STP_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev STP_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev = { NOT = { has_ideology = contractual_etatism } } } add_country_leader_role = { character = STP_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev promote_leader = no country_leader = { ideology = contractual_etatism expire = "2200.1.1.1" } } }
                promote_character = { character = STP_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev ideology = contractual_etatism }
            }
            else = {
                if = { limit = { has_character = STS_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev STS_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev = { NOT = { has_ideology = contractual_etatism } } } add_country_leader_role = { character = STS_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev promote_leader = no country_leader = { ideology = contractual_etatism expire = "2200.1.1.1" } } }
                promote_character = { character = STS_VAL_Andrei_Rudnev ideology = contractual_etatism }
            }
            if = { limit = { has_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy } } remove_power_balance = { id = STP_shabrat_election_legitimacy } }
            load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_administration_focus keep_completed = no }
            mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
            VAL = { country_event = { id = val_contract.347 } }
        }
        if = { limit = { tag = STS } STP_pc_clear_settlement = yes }
        VAL_cede_stelander_border = yes
        set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
    }
}

# Retry at peace or through the existing frontier decisions if a third party
# controlled a border state during the settlement. Never take neutral land.
VAL_cede_stelander_border = {
    if = {
        limit = { is_subject_of = VAL has_cosmetic_tag = STL_VAL_administration }
        # Hosheit becomes an additional Occidian district when OCA already
        # exists under Kefreyt. Otherwise keep the old direct-VAL fallback.
        46 = {
            if = {
                limit = {
                    OR = { is_owned_by = PREV is_owned_by = VAL }
                    OR = { is_controlled_by = PREV is_controlled_by = VAL }
                }
                if = {
                    limit = { OCA = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL has_capitulated = no } }
                    OCA = { transfer_state = 46 }
                    add_core_of = OCA
                    set_state_controller_to = OCA
                    VAL = { VAL_reconcile_occidian_resource_rights = yes }
                }
                else = {
                    VAL = { transfer_state = 46 }
                    set_state_controller_to = VAL
                }
            }
        }
        # Kreyden remains the direct Kefreyt border concession.
        29 = {
            if = {
                limit = { is_owned_by = PREV OR = { is_controlled_by = PREV is_controlled_by = VAL } }
                VAL = { transfer_state = 29 }
                set_state_controller_to = VAL
            }
        }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        OCA = { if = { limit = { exists = yes } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes } }
        VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
    }
}

VAL_install_nodrul_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = NOD VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no } }
		VAL = { VAL_snapshot_nodrul_settlement = yes }
		VAL_settle_nodrul_bezhaysk_war = yes
		VAL_settle_frontier_partner_war = yes
		if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL }
		VAL = { VAL_partition_nodrul_settlement = yes }
		VAL_prepare_nodrul_administration = yes
	}
}

VAL_prepare_nodrul_administration = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = NOD VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no } }
		if = {
			limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
				faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
			}
			else = {
				faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = NOD }
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending
		set_variable = { var = VAL_nodrul_administration_retry value = 0 }
		country_event = { id = val_contract.353 days = 1 }
	}
}


# COUNTRY NOD. The Kefreyt campaign owns this war; it must not remain open
# until Bezhaysk's separate capitulation callback fires.
VAL_settle_nodrul_bezhaysk_war = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            tag = NOD
            has_war_with = BJK
            VAL = { has_country_flag = ADISCORD_bezhaysk_campaign_active }
        }
        white_peace = BJK
    }
}


VAL_finish_nodrul_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            tag = NOD
            has_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending
            is_subject_of = VAL
            VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_cosmetic_tag = NOD_VAL_administration } }
            set_cosmetic_tag = NOD_VAL_administration
            set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
            if = { limit = { has_character = NOD_VAL_Contract_Council NOD_VAL_Contract_Council = { NOT = { has_ideology = contractual_etatism } } } add_country_leader_role = { character = NOD_VAL_Contract_Council promote_leader = no country_leader = { ideology = contractual_etatism expire = "2200.1.1.1" } } }
            promote_character = { character = NOD_VAL_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism }
            load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_administration_focus keep_completed = no }
            mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
            VAL = { country_event = { id = val_contract.348 } }
        }
        clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_entered
        clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_intervention_ready
        clr_country_flag = NOD_cw_intervention_approved
        remove_mission = NOD_cw_intervention_preparation
        remove_mission = NOD_cw_northern_redeployment
        set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending
        clear_variable = VAL_nodrul_administration_retry
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        VAL = {
            VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
    }
}

VAL_complete_nodrul_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            tag = NOD
            has_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending
            VAL = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no is_subject = no }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { is_subject_of = VAL }
            VAL_finish_nodrul_administration = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
            if = {
                limit = { is_faction_leader = yes }
                faction_leader = { dismantle_faction = yes }
            }
            else = {
                faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = NOD }
            }
        }
        else = {
            VAL = {
                set_autonomy = {
                    target = NOD
                    autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration
                    freedom_level = 0.10
                    end_wars = no
                    end_civil_wars = no
                }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { is_subject_of = VAL }
                VAL_finish_nodrul_administration = yes
            }
        }

        # Bound the cache-retry loop. Normally one deferred tick is enough;
        # four attempts leave room for nested peace/faction callbacks.
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending }
            add_to_variable = { var = VAL_nodrul_administration_retry value = 1 }
            if = {
                limit = {
                    check_variable = {
                        var = VAL_nodrul_administration_retry
                        value = 4
                        compare = less_than
                    }
                }
                country_event = { id = val_contract.353 days = 1 }
            }
            else = {
                set_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_failed
                log = "[VAL] Nodrul settlement failed after bounded faction/autonomy retries"
            }
        }
    }
    else = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_nodrul_administration_pending
        clear_variable = VAL_nodrul_administration_retry
    }
}

# VAL scope. Country creation uses only the defeated bloc's captured footprint.
# The three arrays survive white peace and the subsequent change of overlord.
VAL_snapshot_nodrul_settlement = {
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_ECA_states
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_YPR_states
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_DCA_states
    if = {
        limit = { ECA = { exists = no } }
        13 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
        17 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
        18 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
        14 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states value = THIS } } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { YPR = { OR = { exists = no is_subject_of = NOD } } DCA = { exists = no } }
        19 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
        20 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
        21 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
        22 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states value = THIS } } }
        8 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
        15 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
        16 = { if = { limit = { VAL_nod_partition_state_available = yes } add_to_temp_array = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states value = THIS } } }
    }
}

# Each recipient keeps its own army and administration tree after the partition.
VAL_partition_nodrul_settlement = {
    for_each_scope_loop = { array = VAL_nod_ECA_states add_core_of = ECA ECA = { transfer_state = PREV } set_state_controller_to = ECA }
    for_each_scope_loop = { array = VAL_nod_YPR_states add_core_of = YPR YPR = { transfer_state = PREV } set_state_controller_to = YPR }
    for_each_scope_loop = { array = VAL_nod_DCA_states add_core_of = DCA DCA = { transfer_state = PREV } set_state_controller_to = DCA }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_nod_ECA_states^num value = 0 compare = greater_than } ECA = { exists = yes } }
        set_autonomy = { target = ECA autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
        ECA = { inherit_technology = NOD VAL_initialize_partition_administration = yes }
        NOD = { transfer_units_fraction = { target = ECA size = 0.4 stockpile_ratio = 0.4 army_ratio = 0.4 navy_ratio = 0 air_ratio = 0.4 keep_unit_leaders_trigger = { always = yes } } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_nod_DCA_states^num value = 0 compare = greater_than } DCA = { exists = yes } }
        set_autonomy = { target = DCA autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
        DCA = { inherit_technology = YPR VAL_initialize_partition_administration = yes }
        YPR = { transfer_units_fraction = { target = DCA size = 0.4 stockpile_ratio = 0.4 army_ratio = 0.4 navy_ratio = 0 air_ratio = 0.4 keep_unit_leaders_trigger = { always = yes } } }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_nod_YPR_states^num value = 0 compare = greater_than } YPR = { exists = yes } }
        set_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
        YPR = { VAL_initialize_partition_administration = yes }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_nod_DCA_states^num value = 0 compare = greater_than } has_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement VAL_frontier_partner_available = yes YPR = { is_subject_of = VAL } DCA = { is_subject_of = VAL } }
        VAL_transfer_yubora_administrations = yes
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = {
            VAL_frontier_partner_available = yes
            check_variable = { var = VAL_nod_DCA_states^num value = 0 compare = greater_than }
            TFF = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL } }
            NOD = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 2 compare = equals } }
            YPR = { is_subject_of = VAL } DCA = { is_subject_of = VAL }
        }
        set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_frontier_yubora_ultimatum value = 1 days = 30 }
        country_event = { id = val_contract.352 days = 1 }
    }
    clr_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement
    clr_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_ECA_states
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_YPR_states
    clear_temp_array = VAL_nod_DCA_states
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    NOD = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
}

VAL_initialize_partition_administration = {
    set_politics = { ruling_party = etatism elections_allowed = no }
    if = { limit = { tag = ECA } promote_character = { character = ECA_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism } }
    if = { limit = { tag = DCA } promote_character = { character = DCA_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism } }
    if = { limit = { tag = YPR } set_cosmetic_tag = YPR_VAL_administration if = { limit = { has_character = YPR_Contract_Council YPR_Contract_Council = { NOT = { has_ideology = contractual_etatism } } } add_country_leader_role = { character = YPR_Contract_Council promote_leader = no country_leader = { ideology = contractual_etatism expire = "2200.1.1.1" } } } promote_character = { character = YPR_Contract_Council ideology = contractual_etatism } }
    load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_administration_focus keep_completed = yes }
    set_rule = { can_join_factions = no can_create_factions = no }
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

# VAL scope. Freeze the territorial package before the recipient sees its offer.
VAL_open_nod_frontier_offer = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_nod_frontier_offer_current = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending has_country_flag = VAL_campaign_mobilizing } }
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = { flag = VAL_tff_freedom_policy value > 1 } }
            set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending value = 2 days = 15 }
        }
        else = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending value = 1 days = 15 } }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_tff_principles_pending
        TFF = { country_event = { id = val_contract.350 } }
    }
}

VAL_accept_nod_frontier_offer = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_nod_frontier_offer_current = yes has_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending }
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_nod_limited_offer = yes }
            set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement value = 2 }
        }
        else = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement value = 1 } }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_offer_pending
        clr_country_flag = VAL_tff_principles_pending
        VAL_call_frontier_partner = yes
        country_event = { id = val_contract.351 }
    }
}

# VAL scope. A signed limited treaty transfers Iskhait only. An unsigned
# postwar concession retains the ultimatum's original two-administration terms.
VAL_transfer_yubora_administrations = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_partner_available = yes YPR = { is_subject_of = VAL } DCA = { is_subject_of = VAL } }
        NOD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF } }
        ECA = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF } }
        YPR = {
            if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
            if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = TFF } } faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = PREV } }
        }
        DCA = {
            if = { limit = { has_war_with = TFF } white_peace = TFF }
            if = {
                limit = { VAL_nod_limited_agreement = no }
                if = { limit = { is_in_faction = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = TFF } } faction_leader = { remove_from_faction = PREV } }
            }
        }
        TFF = {
            set_autonomy = { target = YPR autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
            if = {
                limit = { VAL_nod_limited_agreement = no }
                set_autonomy = { target = DCA autonomy_state = autonomy_VAL_contract_administration freedom_level = 0.10 end_wars = no end_civil_wars = no }
            }
            if = {
                limit = { is_in_faction = yes }
                faction_leader = {
                    add_to_faction = YPR
                    if = { limit = { DCA = { is_subject_of = TFF } } add_to_faction = DCA }
                }
            }
            VAL_call_frontier_administrations = yes
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        YPR = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
        DCA = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
    clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_yubora_ultimatum
	STP_cw_queue_peace_reconciliation = yes
}

VAL_reject_frontier_ultimatum = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_frontier_ultimatum_current = yes }
        TFF = { declare_war_on = { target = VAL type = annex_everything } }
        VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes
    }
    clr_country_flag = VAL_frontier_yubora_ultimatum
}

# VAL scope. Repeated war callbacks only add relations which are still missing.
VAL_call_frontier_partner = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement
            VAL_frontier_partner_available = yes
            has_war_with = NOD
            NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no }
            TFF = { NOT = { is_in_faction_with = NOD } }
        }
        TFF = {
            if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAL enemy = NOD hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
            if = {
                limit = { has_war_with = NOD }
                if = { limit = { VAL = { has_war_with = STP } STP = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member } NOT = { has_war_with = STP is_in_faction_with = STP } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAL enemy = STP hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
                if = { limit = { VAL = { has_war_with = STS } STS = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member } NOT = { has_war_with = STS is_in_faction_with = STS } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAL enemy = STS hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
                ADISCORD_TFF_begin_kefreyt_campaign = yes
                VAL_call_frontier_administrations = yes
            }
        }
    }
}

# COUNTRY defeated enemy. Close the partner's relations before installing a client.
VAL_settle_frontier_partner_war = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_frontier_agreement } TFF = { has_country_flag = TFF_kefreyt_northern_campaign } has_war_with = TFF }
        white_peace = TFF
    }
}

# TFF scope; only the two administrations transferred by this treaty participate.
VAL_call_frontier_administrations = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = TFF exists = yes is_subject = no has_war = yes }
        every_enemy_country = {
            save_event_target_as = VAL_frontier_administration_enemy
            YPR = {
                if = {
                    limit = { is_subject_of = TFF has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = TFF has_war_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = TFF enemy = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                }
            }
            DCA = {
                if = {
                    limit = { is_subject_of = TFF has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = TFF has_war_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy } }
                    add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = TFF enemy = event_target:VAL_frontier_administration_enemy hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. Apply the Ainholm clause from the pre-capitulation snapshot.
VAL_transfer_party_controlled_ainholm_to_frontier = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            tag = VAL
            TFF = { exists = yes }
            STP = {
                has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
                has_country_flag = VAL_final_party_controlled_ainholm
            }
            118 = { owner = { OR = { tag = AIN tag = NOD tag = STP } } }
            119 = { owner = { OR = { tag = AIN tag = NOD tag = STP } } }
        }
        TFF = {
            transfer_state = 118
            transfer_state = 119
            ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        }
        118 = { set_state_controller_to = TFF }
        119 = { set_state_controller_to = TFF }
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
        log = "[VAL] Party-held Ainholm transferred to the Free Frontier after Kefreyt coalition victory"
    }
}

# COUNTRY VAL; event-driven, bounded to the three final-war recipients.
VAL_finalize_reserved_settlements = {
    # Snapshot every eligible coalition member before the first white peace or
    # autonomy change mutates the shared war. This makes same-faction defeats
    # settle as one transaction instead of invalidating the second member.
    STP = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_final_settlement_ready = yes }
            set_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_party_controlled_ainholm
        }
    }
    STS = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_final_settlement_ready = yes }
            set_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
        }
    }
    NOD = {
        clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_final_settlement_ready = yes }
            set_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate } }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
        }
    }

    # The territorial clause is evaluated while the pre-settlement controllers
    # still exist. Only the Party-side Stelander result can trigger it.
    if = {
        limit = { STP = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit } }
        VAL_transfer_party_controlled_ainholm_to_frontier = yes
    }

    STP = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_party_controlled_ainholm
            VAL_install_stelander_administration = yes
        }
    }
    STS = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
            VAL_install_stelander_administration = yes
        }
    }
    NOD = {
        if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
            # white_peace dispatches on_peace before the subject is installed.
            VAL_install_nodrul_administration = yes
            clr_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit
        }
    }
}


# DEBUG-only caller: emulate a decisive VAL victory without bypassing the
# production Nodrul settlement. The transient capitulation flag is consumed by
# the same reservation/finalization code as the native callback.
VAL_debug_force_nodrul_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			NOD = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL }
		}
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 }
		NOD = {
			set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
			set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
			set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate days = 1 }
		}
		VAL_finalize_reserved_settlements = yes
		NOD = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate }
	}
}

# DEBUG-only QA router. This does not annex countries directly: it selects the
# same settlement effects used by the production capitulation callbacks.
VAL_debug_force_current_war_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } }
		VAL_frontier_settle_victory = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts
			NOD = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL has_war_with = STS }
		}
		VAL_settle_joint_nod_shabrat_victory = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 }
		STP = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL }
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
				set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate days = 1 }
			}
		}
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL }
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
				set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate days = 1 }
			}
		}
		NOD = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL }
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
				set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate days = 1 }
			}
		}
		VAL_finalize_reserved_settlements = yes
		STP = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate }
		STS = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate }
		NOD = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate }
	}
}

VAL_adopt_commonwealth = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_can_proclaim_commonwealth = yes }
        set_cosmetic_tag = VAL_commonwealth
        VAL_sync_solgalov_portrait = yes
        VAL_grant_contract_warlord = yes
    }
}

# Country VAL. Initialize once; loading a save must preserve earned progress.
VAL_initialize_arsenal_recovery = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_active OR = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started 33 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = WRK is_controlled_by = WRK } } } }
        VAL_end_vorkerland_resource_access = yes
    }
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage } }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage value = 4 }
        if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted } set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage value = 0 } }
    }
    VAL_refresh_arsenal_reputation = yes
    if = {
        limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_initialized } }
        set_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_initialized
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_global_flag = ADISCORD_vorkerland_collapse_wars_started } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted } WRK = { exists = yes } 33 = { is_owned_by = WRK is_controlled_by = WRK } }
            WRK = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 33 } }
            set_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_active
        }
    }
    VAL_refresh_industrial_economy = yes
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_contracts_disrupted } mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes }

}

VAL_end_vorkerland_resource_access = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_active }
        remove_resource_rights = 33
        clr_country_flag = VAL_vorkerland_resource_access_active
        VAL_assert_vorkerland_metal_shortage = yes
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
    VAL_handle_westerholm_loss = yes
}

VAL_refresh_arsenal_reputation = {
    clamp_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage min = 0 max = 5 }
    set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_trade value = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage }
    multiply_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_trade value = 0.05 }
    add_to_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_trade value = -0.20 }
    set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_credit value = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage }
    multiply_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_credit value = 0.025 }
    add_to_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_credit value = -0.10 }
    set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_quality value = 0 }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage value = 5 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } set_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_quality value = 0.10 } }
    if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = VAL_arsenal_reputation } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_arsenal_reputation } }
    force_update_dynamic_modifier = yes
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

VAL_restore_arsenal_reputation = {
    effect_tooltip = { add_ideas = VAL_arsenal_reputation_delta }
    hidden_effect = {
        add_to_variable = { var = VAL_arsenal_reputation_stage value = 1 }
        VAL_refresh_arsenal_reputation = yes
    }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. Native relation callbacks can recurse when a subject joins;
# one synchronous guard keeps each enemy/subject pair within this dispatch.
VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = VAL
			exists = yes
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_subject_war_dispatch_active }
		}
		set_country_flag = VAL_subject_war_dispatch_active

		# A Kefreyt client cannot fight a private war while its overlord remains neutral.
		# Adopt subject wars first, then fan every VAL war back out to the whole client network.
		every_subject_country = {
			limit = {
				has_capitulated = no
				has_war = yes
				NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
			}
			save_event_target_as = VAL_subject_war_leader
			every_enemy_country = {
				limit = {
					has_capitulated = no
					NOT = { tag = VAL }
					NOT = { is_subject_of = VAL }
					NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL }
					VAL = { NOT = { has_war_with = PREV } }
				}
				save_event_target_as = VAL_subject_war_enemy
				VAL = {
					if = {
						limit = {
							NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy }
							NOT = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy }
						}
						add_to_war = {
							targeted_alliance = event_target:VAL_subject_war_leader
							enemy = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy
							hostility_reason = asked_to_join
							single_target_only = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			every_enemy_country = {
				save_event_target_as = VAL_subject_war_enemy
				every_subject_country = {
					limit = {
						has_capitulated = no
						NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
						NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL }
					}
					add_to_war = {
						targeted_alliance = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy
						enemy = VAL
						hostility_reason = asked_to_join
						single_target_only = yes
					}
				}
				VAL = {
					every_subject_country = {
						limit = {
							has_capitulated = no
							NOT = { has_war_with = VAL }
							NOT = { has_war_with = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy }
							NOT = { is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy }
						}
						add_to_war = {
							targeted_alliance = VAL
							enemy = event_target:VAL_subject_war_enemy
							hostility_reason = asked_to_join
							single_target_only = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = VAL_subject_war_dispatch_active
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL: native extraction rights cover one of the two island deposits.
VAL_deliver_nam_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_nam_concession_deliverable = yes }
		NAM = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 230 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		set_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_granted
        NAM = { VAL_contract_record_success = yes }
		set_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active
		clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_offer_pending
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_cancel_nam_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active }
		remove_resource_rights = 230
		clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		NAM = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
	clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_offer_pending
	clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_agreed
	clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_aid_delivered
}

VAL_check_nam_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_agreed has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active }
			OR = {
				has_war_with = NAM
				NAM = { exists = no }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_coalition_victory
				230 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = NAM } }
				AND = { has_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_active 230 = { NOT = { is_controlled_by = NAM } } }
			}
		}
		VAL_cancel_nam_concession = yes
	}
}


VAL_grant_contract_warlord = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            tag = VAL
            has_cosmetic_tag = VAL_commonwealth
            has_country_leader = { character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov ruling_only = yes }
        }
        VAL_Valera_Solgalov = {
            if = {
                limit = { NOT = { has_trait = VAL_contract_warlord } }
                add_country_leader_trait = { ideology = contractual_etatism trait = VAL_contract_warlord }
            }
        }
    }
}


VAL_initialize_balchansk_administration = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = OCA exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL }
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_focus_tree = VAL_administration_focus } }
            load_focus_tree = { tree = VAL_administration_focus keep_completed = yes }
            mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
        }
        VAL_reconcile_balchansk_income = yes
        ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    }
}


VAL_reconcile_balchansk_income = {
    if = {
        limit = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL has_completed_focus = OCA_harbour_customs owns_state = 44 controls_state = 44 }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = OCA_harbour_customs_service } } add_ideas = OCA_harbour_customs_service }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { has_idea = OCA_harbour_customs_service } remove_ideas = OCA_harbour_customs_service }
    if = {
        limit = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL has_completed_focus = OCA_mining_concessions owns_state = 88 controls_state = 88 }
        if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = OCA_mining_concession_income } } add_ideas = OCA_mining_concession_income }
    }
    else_if = { limit = { has_idea = OCA_mining_concession_income } remove_ideas = OCA_mining_concession_income }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. The war edge reveals the branch; startup repairs its visibility
# in existing campaigns. The briefing marker records delivery, not availability.
VAL_reveal_stelander_focus_branch = {
    if = {
        limit = { tag = VAL exists = yes has_focus_tree = VAL_focus has_global_flag = STP_cw_started }
        mark_focus_tree_layout_dirty = yes
        if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_focus_briefed } }
            set_country_flag = VAL_stelander_focus_briefed
            if = {
                limit = { is_ai = no NOT = { has_completed_focus = VAL_Stelander_Crisis_Opens } }
                country_event = { id = val_rework.9 hours = 1 }
            }
        }
    }
}

# Called once by the fresh-campaign initializer. These memberships are permanent,
# so campaign cleanup never records or removes them as temporary participants.
VAL_initialize_northern_bloc = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            CIN = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no }
            OSF = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no }
            APH = { exists = yes is_subject = no is_in_faction = no }
        }
        CIN = {
            create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = VAL_frontier_defence_coalition }
            add_to_faction = OSF
            add_to_faction = APH
        }
    }
}

# VAL: start the clock when the ultimatum arrives, independent of the event option.
VAL_frontier_issue_nod_ultimatum = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals }
            check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = less_than }
            OR = { has_war_with = CIN has_war_with = OSF has_war_with = APH }
            NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_issued }
            VAL_stelander_dominated = yes
            NOD = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_subject = no is_subject_of = STP } NOT = { has_war_with = VAL is_in_faction_with = VAL has_war_with = CIN has_war_with = OSF has_war_with = APH } }
        }
        set_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_issued
        activate_mission = VAL_nod_ultimatum
        country_event = { id = val_rework.118 }
    }
}

VAL_frontier_expire_nod_ultimatum = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 3 compare = equals } has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_issued }
        # Resolve an already earned victory before testing intervention on the same day.
        if = {
            limit = { VAL_frontier_target_occupied = yes VAL_frontier_members_beaten = yes }
            VAL_frontier_settle_victory = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_nod_ultimatum_expired }
            VAL_frontier_join_existing_war = yes
        }
    }
}

# Final campaign: 1 preparation, 2 war, 3 victory, 4 closed. Only VAL's weekly
# hook advances escalation; startup and war callbacks never advance the clock.
VAL_final_crisis_initialize = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_final_crisis_phase } VAL_final_crisis_available = yes VAL_frontier_idle = yes }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_escalation value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_supply_bonus value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_planning_bonus value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_reserve_share value = 0 }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_final_war_preparation }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_refugee_nodrul_admitted value = 3 compare = less_than } }
			set_country_flag = VAL_refugee_nodrul_seen
			set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_refugee_nodrul_window days = 180 }
		}
		unlock_decision_tooltip = VAL_final_register_reserves
		unlock_decision_tooltip = VAL_final_stockpile_supplies
		unlock_decision_tooltip = VAL_final_reconnaissance
	}
}

# COUNTRY participant. Preserve major status which belonged to another system.
VAL_final_crisis_register_member = {
	set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
	if = { limit = { is_major = no } set_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major set_major = yes }
}

VAL_final_crisis_join_war = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_war_with = NOD NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 compare = equals } } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 }
		NOD = { VAL_final_crisis_register_member = yes }
		STP = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_in_faction_with = NOD is_subject_of = NOD NOD = { is_subject_of = STP } } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = NOD enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
				if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } VAL_final_crisis_register_member = yes }
			}
		}
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no OR = { is_in_faction_with = NOD is_subject_of = NOD } NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL is_subject_of = VAL } }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = NOD enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } }
				if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } VAL_final_crisis_register_member = yes }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { NOT = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_final_war_preparation } } } add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_final_war_preparation } }
		VAL_call_frontier_partner = yes
		VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes
	}
}

VAL_final_crisis_launch = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_final_crisis_preparing = yes }
		NOD = { VAL_final_crisis_register_member = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { NOD = { is_subject_of = STP } }
			STP = { VAL_final_crisis_register_member = yes }
			declare_war_on = { target = STP type = annex_everything }
			NOD = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = STP enemy = VAL hostility_reason = asked_to_join single_target_only = yes } } }
		}
		else = { declare_war_on = { target = NOD type = annex_everything } }
		VAL_final_crisis_join_war = yes
	}
}

VAL_final_refund_supply_order = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_final_supply_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAL_final_supply_deposit }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = VAL_final_supply_deposit }
		clear_variable = VAL_final_supply_deposit
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

VAL_final_crisis_close = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 4 }
	if = { limit = { NOD = { OR = { is_subject_of = VAL has_country_flag = VAL_final_settlement_commit has_country_flag = VAL_final_capitulation_immediate } } has_capitulated = no is_subject = no } set_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 3 } }
	if = { limit = { has_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_final_war_preparation } } remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = VAL_final_war_preparation } }
	VAL_final_refund_supply_order = yes

	NOD = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } }
	STP = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } }
	STS = { clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } set_major = no clr_country_flag = VAL_final_war_added_major } }
}

VAL_final_crisis_reconcile = {
	# Save-safe repair for campaigns created before the Stelander-first gate.
	# An old final NOD war must not become an untracked permanent war after load.
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_war_with = NOD
			VAL_stelander_dominated = no
			OR = {
				check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 compare = equals }
				NOD = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member }
			}
			NOD = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_frontier_guarantor } }
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts }
		}
		NOD = { if = { limit = { has_war_with = VAL } white_peace = VAL } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 0 compare = greater_than } }
			VAL_final_crisis_close = yes
		}
	}

	# Recover a missed immediate-capitulation callback in a valid final campaign.
	# Defeated registered members stay reserved until every required ally is down.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 compare = equals }
			VAL_stelander_dominated = yes
			OR = {
				has_war_with = NOD
				NOD = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member has_capitulated = yes }
			}
			NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts }
		}
		NOD = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member
					has_capitulated = yes
					NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending }
				}
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
			}
		}
		STP = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_war_with = VAL has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member has_capitulated = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending } }
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
			}
		}
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_war_with = VAL has_country_flag = VAL_final_war_member has_capitulated = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending } }
				set_country_flag = VAL_final_defeat_pending
			}
		}
		VAL_finalize_reserved_settlements = yes
	}

	# The shared Shabrat campaign owns its own peace. Recover it separately if
	# the immediate capitulation callback was skipped by an older save.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 2 compare = equals }
			has_country_flag = VAL_joint_nod_campaign_with_sts
			NOD = { exists = yes has_war_with = VAL has_war_with = STS has_capitulated = yes }
			STS = {
				exists = yes
				has_capitulated = no
				has_country_leader = { character = STP_maksim_shabrat ruling_only = yes }
			}
		}
		VAL_settle_joint_nod_shabrat_victory = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 0 compare = greater_than } check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 3 compare = less_than } }
		if = { limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes is_subject = yes has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_defeated } } VAL_final_crisis_close = yes }
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 1 compare = equals } }
			if = { limit = { has_war_with = NOD VAL_final_campaign_separate_from_frontier = yes } VAL_final_crisis_join_war = yes }
			else_if = { limit = { VAL_final_crisis_available = no } VAL_final_crisis_close = yes }
		}
		else_if = { limit = { NOT = { OR = { has_war_with = NOD has_war_with = STP has_war_with = STS } } } VAL_final_crisis_close = yes }
	}

	# Postwar repair is intentionally retried by the weekly reconciliation pass.
	# This covers both fresh settlements and development saves loaded after a
	# previously completed final war, without relaxing any territorial requirement.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_final_crisis_phase value = 3 compare = greater_than_or_equals }
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			NOT = { has_cosmetic_tag = VAL_commonwealth }
		}
		if = { limit = { VAL_can_form_northern_administration = yes } VAL_form_northern_administration = yes }
		STP = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL has_cosmetic_tag = STL_VAL_administration }
				VAL_cede_stelander_border = yes
			}
		}
		STS = {
			if = {
				limit = { exists = yes is_subject_of = VAL has_cosmetic_tag = STL_VAL_administration }
				VAL_cede_stelander_border = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

VAL_final_crisis_weekly = {
	VAL_final_crisis_initialize = yes
	VAL_final_crisis_reconcile = yes
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_final_crisis_preparing = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_final_escalation value = 5 }
		clamp_variable = { var = VAL_final_escalation min = 0 max = 100 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_final_escalation value = 100 compare = greater_than_or_equals } } VAL_final_crisis_launch = yes }
	}
}


# The cosmetic state is authoritative, including when repairing an older save.
VAL_sync_solgalov_portrait = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov }
        if = {
            limit = { has_cosmetic_tag = VAL_commonwealth }
            set_portraits = {
                character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov
                civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_VAL_Valera_Solgalov_contracts }
            }
        }
        else = {
            set_portraits = {
                character = VAL_Valera_Solgalov
                civilian = { large = GFX_portrait_VAL_Valera_Solgalov }
            }
        }
    }
}

# Only an explicit prewar withdrawal selects the alternative route.
# The bilateral Irem claim does not determine the northern policy.
VAL_frontier_withdraw_and_defer = {
    if = {
        limit = {
            has_war = no
            check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_stage value = 2 compare = equals }
        }
        if = {
            limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_frontier_target value = 4 compare = less_than } }
            set_country_flag = VAL_frontier_expansion_deferred
        }
        VAL_frontier_close = yes
    }
}

VAL_council_begin_revanche = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_council_can_launch_revanche = yes }
		set_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active
		declare_war_on = { target = STS type = annex_everything }
		hidden_effect = { VAL_call_subjects_to_wars = yes }
		if = {
			limit = { has_war_with = STS }
			STS = { country_event = { id = ADISCORD_STP_pc.32 } }
		}
		else = { clr_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active }
	}
}

# COUNTRY VAL: a separately arranged peace must not mark an unrelated later war.
VAL_council_reconcile_revanche = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active NOT = { has_war_with = STS } }
		clr_country_flag = VAL_council_revanche_active
	}
}

# Idempotent staff delivery. The receipt survives retirement and annexation.
VAL_qol_initialize_staff = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_qol_staff_available = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_qol_regular_officers_delivered } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_qol_regular_officers_delivered
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Anton_Rein }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = VAL_Anton_Rein
				traits = { commando }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Irina_Karst }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = VAL_Irina_Karst
				traits = { organizer }
				skill = 2
				attack_skill = 2
				defense_skill = 2
				planning_skill = 2
				logistics_skill = 2
			}
		}
	}
	if = { limit = { VAL_qol_elite_army_available = yes } VAL_qol_grant_elite_officer = yes }
}

# Called directly by completion rewards; never tests its own finishing focus.
VAL_qol_grant_elite_officer = {
	if = {
		limit = { VAL_qol_staff_available = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_qol_elite_officer_delivered } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_qol_elite_officer_delivered
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Viktor_Bran }
			add_corps_commander_role = {
				character = VAL_Viktor_Bran
				traits = { panzer_leader }
				skill = 4
				attack_skill = 4
				defense_skill = 4
				planning_skill = 4
				logistics_skill = 4
			}
		}
	}
}

# Existing commanders provide named opposition figures; their command roles stay intact.
VAL_qol_initialize_party_leaders = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = VAL NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_qol_party_leaders_initialized } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_defeated } }
		set_country_flag = VAL_qol_party_leaders_initialized
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Boris_Gromov NOT = { has_government = chauvinism } }
			add_country_leader_role = { character = VAL_Boris_Gromov promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = chauvinism_ideology expire = "2200.1.1.1" } }
			promote_character = { character = VAL_Boris_Gromov ideology = chauvinism_ideology }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_character = VAL_Renata_Morn NOT = { has_government = pragmatism } }
			add_country_leader_role = { character = VAL_Renata_Morn promote_leader = yes country_leader = { ideology = pragmatism_ideology expire = "2200.1.1.1" } }
			promote_character = { character = VAL_Renata_Morn ideology = pragmatism_ideology }
		}
	}
}


# COUNTRY VAL: recognition of political equality ends any remaining territorial
# claim on Stelander's national territory. Only states actually owned by VAL move.
VAL_return_stelander_cores = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = VAL }
  every_owned_state = {
   limit = {
    OR = { is_core_of = STP is_core_of = STS }
   }
   if = {
    limit = { STS = { exists = yes } is_core_of = STS }
    transfer_state_to = STS
    set_state_controller_to = STS
   }
   else_if = {
    limit = { STP = { exists = yes } is_core_of = STP }
    transfer_state_to = STP
    set_state_controller_to = STP
   }
  }
  ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
 }
}

VAL_offer_stelander_equal_alliance = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = VAL VAL_stelander_equal_partner_available = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_alliance_offer_pending } }
  set_country_flag = VAL_stelander_alliance_offer_pending
  if = {
   limit = { STS = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_equal_recognition } }
   STS = { country_event = { id = val_contract.354 days = 1 } }
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { STP = { exists = yes has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_equal_recognition } }
   STP = { country_event = { id = val_contract.354 days = 1 } }
  }
 }
}

VAL_form_equal_stelander_alliance = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = VAL }
  if = {
   limit = { NOT = { is_in_faction = yes } }
   create_faction_from_template = { template = faction_template_ADISCORD_standard name = VAL_stelander_equal_alliance_name }
  }
  if = {
   limit = { STS = { exists = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL } } }
   add_to_faction = STS
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { STP = { exists = yes NOT = { is_in_faction_with = VAL } } }
   add_to_faction = STP
  }
 }
}

VAL_call_stelander_partner_to_wars = {
 if = {
  limit = { tag = VAL has_country_flag = VAL_stelander_equal_alliance }
  if = {
   limit = { has_war_with = NOD STS = { exists = yes is_in_faction_with = VAL NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } } }
   STS = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAL enemy = NOD single_target_only = no } }
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { has_war_with = NOD STP = { exists = yes is_in_faction_with = VAL NOT = { has_war_with = NOD } } }
   STP = { add_to_war = { targeted_alliance = VAL enemy = NOD single_target_only = no } }
  }
 }
}

# --- War aid contracts ---
# Aid state: 1 collecting, 2 fulfilled, -1 deadline missed, 3 settled.

VAL_start_resource_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_resource_aid_state } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_rifles value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_personnel value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_volunteer_days value = 0 }
		activate_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline
		add_ideas = VAL_resource_volunteer_mandate
	}
}

# Called by mission success as well as direct deliveries; never removes its own mission.
VAL_complete_resource_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } VAL_resource_aid_sufficient = yes }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 2 }
		if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 1 compare = equals } } set_country_flag = VAL_nam_aid_delivered }
	}
}

VAL_fail_resource_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = -1 }
		country_event = { id = val_contract.371 }
	}
}

VAL_deliver_resource_aid_arms = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			has_active_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline
			check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals }
			FROM = { VAL_resource_aid_recipient = yes }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 25000 } }
		}
		send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = 25000 target = FROM }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_rifles value = 25000 }
		VAL_complete_resource_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_deliver_resource_aid_personnel = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			has_active_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline
			check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals }
			FROM = { VAL_resource_aid_recipient = yes }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 2000 } NOT = { command_power < 25 }
		}
		add_manpower = -2000
		add_command_power = -25
		FROM = { add_manpower = 2000 add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_paid_military_advisors days = 70 } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_personnel value = 2000 }
		VAL_complete_resource_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_start_northern_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = VAL_northern_aid_state } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 1 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_rifles value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_personnel value = 0 }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_volunteer_days value = 0 }
		activate_mission = VAL_northern_aid_deadline

	}
}

# Called by mission success as well as direct deliveries; never removes its own mission.
VAL_complete_northern_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } VAL_northern_aid_sufficient = yes }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 2 }

	}
}

VAL_fail_northern_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = -1 }
		country_event = { id = val_contract.371 }
	}
}

VAL_deliver_northern_aid_arms = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			VAL_northern_aid_deliveries_open = yes
			FROM = { VAL_northern_aid_recipient = yes }
			NOT = { has_equipment = { infantry_equipment < 25000 } }
		}
		send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = 25000 target = FROM }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_rifles value = 25000 }
		VAL_complete_northern_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_deliver_northern_aid_personnel = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_capitulated = no
			is_subject = no
			VAL_northern_aid_deliveries_open = yes
			FROM = { VAL_northern_aid_recipient = yes }
			NOT = { has_manpower < 2000 } NOT = { command_power < 25 }
		}
		add_manpower = -2000
		add_command_power = -25
		FROM = { add_manpower = 2000 add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_paid_military_advisors days = 70 } }
		add_to_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_personnel value = 2000 }
		VAL_complete_northern_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_resource_aid_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_resource_aid_state NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_active = no }
			VAL_settle_resource_aid = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_capitulated = yes
					is_subject = yes
					AND = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 1 compare = equals } has_war_with = NAM }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 2 compare = equals } has_war_with = EFL }
				}
			}
			set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 3 }
			remove_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline
			remove_ideas = VAL_resource_volunteer_mandate
			VAL_cancel_nam_concession = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals } has_active_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						NAM = { VAL_resource_aid_recipient = yes has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 1 } }
						EFL = { VAL_resource_aid_recipient = yes has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = VAL count > 1 } }
					}
				}
				add_to_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_volunteer_days value = 1 }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_volunteer_days value = 0 } }
			VAL_complete_resource_aid = yes
		}
	}
}

# Called after the territorial settlement: EFL may only grant a deposit it now owns.
VAL_settle_resource_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_resource_aid_state NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		VAL_complete_resource_aid = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_capitulated = no
				is_subject = no
				check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 1 compare = equals }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_nam_victory
			}
			VAL_deliver_nam_concession = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				has_capitulated = no
				is_subject = no
				check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 2 compare = equals }
				check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 2 compare = equals }
				has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_coalition_victory
				NOT = { has_war_with = EFL }
				EFL = { exists = yes is_subject = no has_capitulated = no }
				230 = { is_owned_by = EFL is_controlled_by = EFL }
			}
			EFL = { give_resource_rights = { receiver = VAL state = 230 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
			set_country_flag = VAL_efl_concession_active
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals }
				OR = {
					AND = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 1 compare = equals } has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_nam_victory }
					AND = { check_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_side value = 2 compare = equals } has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_coalition_victory }
				}
			}
			VAL_fail_resource_aid = yes
		}
		set_variable = { var = VAL_resource_aid_state value = 3 }
		remove_mission = VAL_resource_aid_deadline
		remove_ideas = VAL_resource_volunteer_mandate
		clr_country_flag = VAL_nam_concession_offer_pending
		clr_country_flag = VAL_efl_concession_offer_pending
	}
}

VAL_check_efl_concession = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = VAL_efl_concession_active
			OR = { has_war_with = EFL EFL = { exists = no } 230 = { NOT = { is_owned_by = EFL is_controlled_by = EFL } } }
		}
		remove_resource_rights = 230
		clr_country_flag = VAL_efl_concession_active
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		EFL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
	}
}

# Startup-only compatibility: a fulfilled pledge cannot lose before the campaign ends.
VAL_restore_fulfilled_northern_aid = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals }
			has_idea = VAL_northern_aid_failure
			VAL_northern_aid_sufficient = yes
			NOD = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 1 compare = equals } }
		}
		remove_ideas = VAL_northern_aid_failure
		set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 2 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
	}
}

# The campaign result is authoritative; individual capitulations precede its settlement.
VAL_northern_aid_daily = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_northern_aid_state NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		if = {
			limit = { NOD = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 2 compare = equals } } }
			VAL_settle_northern_aid_defeat = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { NOD = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 3 compare = equals } } }
			VAL_settle_northern_aid_victory = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes is_subject = yes NOD = { check_variable = { var = STP_cw_northern_campaign_status value = 4 compare = equals } } } }
			VAL_close_northern_aid = yes
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					AND = {
						check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 1 compare = equals }
						has_active_mission = VAL_northern_aid_deadline
					}
					check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 2 compare = equals }
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = { VAL_northern_aid_volunteers_present = yes }
				add_to_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_volunteer_days value = 1 }
			}
			else = { set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_volunteer_days value = 0 } }
			VAL_complete_northern_aid = yes
		}
	}
}


VAL_settle_northern_aid_victory = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_northern_aid_state NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		VAL_complete_northern_aid = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_capitulated = no is_subject = no check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 2 compare = equals } }
			add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_northern_aid_success days = 180 }
			ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
			country_event = { id = val_contract.372 }
		}
		else = { VAL_fail_northern_aid = yes }
		VAL_close_northern_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_settle_northern_aid_defeat = {
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = VAL_northern_aid_state NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 3 compare = equals } } }
		add_timed_idea = { idea = VAL_northern_aid_failure days = 180 }
		ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
		country_event = { id = val_contract.373 }
		VAL_close_northern_aid = yes
	}
}

VAL_close_northern_aid = {
	set_variable = { var = VAL_northern_aid_state value = 3 }
	remove_mission = VAL_northern_aid_deadline
	clear_global_event_target = VAL_northern_aid_partner
}


VAL_refresh_order_summary = {
