 set_variable = { var = VAL_route_occidia_orders value = 0 }
 set_variable = { var = VAL_route_north_orders value = 0 }
 set_variable = { var = VAL_route_stelander_orders value = 0 }
 set_variable = { var = VAL_route_vorkerland_orders value = 0 }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { OR = { tag = CIN tag = OSF tag = APH } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_occidia_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { OR = { tag = NOD tag = YPR tag = COF tag = TFF } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_north_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS tag = SRP } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_stelander_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_vorkerland_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { OR = { tag = CIN tag = OSF tag = APH } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_occidia_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { OR = { tag = NOD tag = YPR tag = COF tag = TFF } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_north_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { OR = { tag = STP tag = STS tag = SRP } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_stelander_orders value = 1 } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { OR = { tag = WRK tag = WKR tag = VAD tag = TVA } } } add_to_variable = { var = VAL_route_vorkerland_orders value = 1 } }
}

# --- external_order_ledger ---
# Orders retain their quote; mission callbacks only mark expiry for the next VAL pulse.
VAL_contract_record_success = {
    add_to_variable = { var = VAL_completed_contracts value = 1 }
    clamp_variable = { var = VAL_completed_contracts min = 0 max = 2 }
}
VAL_close_order_offer = {
    if = {
        limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_offer }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_offer
        clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_offer
        VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_partner_offer_pending clr_country_flag = VAL_export_offer_pending }
    }
}
VAL_order_1_legacy_cancel = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
        event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { add_to_variable = { var = VAL_refund_due value = VAL.VAL_order_1_advance } }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 0 }
        remove_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
    }
 VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes
}

# COUNTRY VAL. Settle order payments with literal tariffs so buyer debit and seller receipt
# cannot depend on a cross-scope variable expression at runtime.
VAL_order_1_settle_payment = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 375 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 375 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 375 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 375 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 375 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 500 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 750 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 750 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 750 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 750 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 750 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 1000 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1000 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1000 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 1687.5 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1687.5 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1687.5 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1687.5 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1687.5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 2250 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 2250 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 2250 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 2250 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 2250 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 3750 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 3750 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 3750 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 3750 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 3750 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 5000 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 5000 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 5000 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 5000 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 5000 }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

VAL_order_2_settle_payment = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 375 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 375 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 375 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 375 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 375 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 500 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 500 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 750 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 750 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 750 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 750 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 750 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 1000 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1000 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1000 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1000 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 1687.5 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1687.5 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1687.5 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1687.5 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1687.5 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 2250 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 2250 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 2250 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 2250 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 2250 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 3750 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 3750 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 3750 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 3750 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 3750 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 5000 compare = equals } }
		event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 5000 } add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 5000 } ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 5000 }
		add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 5000 }
	}
	ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
}

VAL_order_1_legacy_dispatch = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_order_1_can_dispatch = yes }
        VAL_order_1_settle_payment = yes
        event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { VAL_contract_record_success = yes }
        send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_order_1_quantity target = event_target:VAL_order_1_partner }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 2 }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 0 }
        remove_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
        set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_order_1_processing days = 14 }
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
    }
 VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes
}

VAL_order_1_legacy_reconcile = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
        if = {
            limit = { OR = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired has_capitulated = yes NOT = { event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } } } }
            VAL_order_1_cancel = yes
        }
        else_if = {
            limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used } NOT = { event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { VAL_order_partner_route_open = yes } } }
            set_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
            add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 14 }
        }
    }
    else_if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 2 compare = equals } NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_processing } }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 }
    }
}
# COUNTRY buyer. Entry is rechecked before either party is debited.
VAL_accept_order_1 = {
    save_global_event_target_as = VAL_order_1_partner
    ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
    VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 1 compare = equals } }
        subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 125 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 125 }
        VAL = {
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 125 }
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 125 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 25000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 500 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 125 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 375 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
            activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
            add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 0 }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 2 compare = equals } }
        subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 250 }
        VAL = {
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 250 }
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 250 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 50000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 1000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 250 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 750 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
            activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
            add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 15 }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 3 compare = equals } }
        subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 562.5 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 562.5 }
        VAL = {
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 562.5 }
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 562.5 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 100000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 2250 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 562.5 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 1687.5 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
            activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
            add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 30 }
        }
    }
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 4 compare = equals } }
        subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1250 }
        add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 1250 }
        VAL = {
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 1250 }
            add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 1250 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 200000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 50000 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 1250 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 3750 }
            set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 }
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
            clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
            activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
            add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 60 }
        }
    }
    ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
    VAL = { ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes }
    VAL = { VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes }
    VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
 VAL_close_order_offer = yes
}
VAL_order_2_legacy_cancel = {
    if = {
        limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
        event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { add_to_variable = { var = VAL_refund_due value = VAL.VAL_order_2_advance } }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 0 }
        set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_advance value = 0 }
        remove_mission = VAL_order_2_deadline
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_expired
        clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_grace_used
    }
 VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes
}

VAL_accept_legacy_order = {
    if = {
        limit = { VAL_order_can_accept = yes }
        if = { limit = { VAL = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } } NOT = { has_idea = VAL_export_income_1 } } } VAL_accept_order_1 = yes }
        else = { VAL_accept_order_2 = yes }
    }
}
VAL_contract_reconcile = {
    VAL_reconcile_trade_fees = yes
    VAL_reconcile_hire_fees = yes
    VAL_reconcile_advisor_conflict = yes
    VAL_military_reconcile = yes
    VAL_order_1_reconcile = yes
    VAL_order_2_reconcile = yes
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly VAL_order_1_can_dispatch = yes } VAL_order_1_dispatch = yes }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_quarterly VAL_order_2_can_dispatch = yes } VAL_order_2_dispatch = yes }
}

# COUNTRY VAL. Legacy capacity ideas and new receipts must never share a slot.
VAL_record_instant_export = {
 if = {
  limit = { NOT = { has_idea = VAL_export_income_1 } NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } } }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 2 }
  set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_order_1_processing days = 14 }
 }
 else = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 2 } set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_order_2_processing days = 14 } }
}

# --- military_contracts ---
# COUNTRY customer. Capture the conflict before displaying the offer.
VAL_prepare_military_quote = {
 save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_customer
 if = { limit = { VAL_northern_volunteer_host = yes } NOD = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_enemy } }
 else_if = { limit = { tag = EFL } NAM = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_enemy } }
 else_if = { limit = { tag = NAM has_war_with = EFL } EFL = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_enemy } }
 else = { AZH = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_enemy } }
 capital_scope = { save_global_event_target_as = VAL_military_target }
}
VAL_close_military_offer = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_military_offer }
  clr_country_flag = VAL_military_offer
  VAL = { clr_country_flag = VAL_military_offer_pending }
 }
}
VAL_accept_military_contract = {
 if = {
  limit = { VAL_military_offer_valid = yes }
  ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
  subtract_from_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 500 }
  add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_costs value = 500 }
  ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
  VAL = {
   ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
   set_variable = { var = VAL_military_escrow value = 375 }
   set_variable = { var = VAL_military_state value = 1 }
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = 125 }
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = 125 }
   add_command_power = -25
   clr_country_flag = VAL_military_success
   clr_country_flag = VAL_military_expired
   activate_mission = VAL_military_deadline
   if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_goal value = 3 compare = equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_military_deadline days = 90 } }
   ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
  }
  VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
 VAL_close_military_offer = yes
 }
}
# COUNTRY VAL. Escrow is removed from the customer once and settled once.
VAL_military_settle = {
 if = {
  limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_military_success }
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAL_military_escrow }
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_current_month_action_income value = VAL_military_escrow }
   event_target:VAL_military_customer = { VAL_contract_record_success = yes }
  }
  else = {
   add_to_variable = { var = ADISCORD_economy_treasury value = VAL_military_escrow }
   event_target:VAL_military_customer = {
    add_to_variable = { var = VAL_refund_due value = VAL.VAL_military_escrow }
   }
  }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_military_escrow value = 0 }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_military_state value = 0 }
  remove_mission = VAL_military_deadline
  clr_country_flag = VAL_military_expired
  ADISCORD_economy_mark_dirty = yes
 }
}
# Called before peace handlers remove relations. A third-party capitulation is not our victory.
VAL_military_capture_result = {
 if = {
  limit = {
   tag = event_target:VAL_military_enemy
   VAL = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_state value = 1 compare = equals } event_target:VAL_military_customer = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = ROOT } }
   FROM = { OR = { tag = VAL tag = event_target:VAL_military_customer is_in_faction_with = event_target:VAL_military_customer } }
  }
  VAL = { set_country_flag = VAL_military_success }
 }
}
VAL_military_reconcile = {
 if = {
  limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_state value = 1 compare = equals } }
  if = { limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes event_target:VAL_military_customer = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes has_war_with = VAL exists = no } } } } clr_country_flag = VAL_military_success VAL_military_settle = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_military_success } VAL_military_settle = yes }
  else_if = {
   limit = { NOT = { VAL_military_conflict_open = yes } }
   if = {
    limit = { OR = {
     AND = { event_target:VAL_military_customer = { tag = NAM } has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_nam_victory }
     AND = { event_target:VAL_military_customer = { tag = EFL } has_global_flag = ADISCORD_nam_resource_war_coalition_victory }
    } }
    set_country_flag = VAL_military_success
   }
   VAL_military_settle = yes
  }
  else_if = { limit = { NOT = { VAL_military_participating = yes } } VAL_military_settle = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_goal value = 1 compare = equals } NOT = { VAL_military_target_secured = yes } } VAL_military_settle = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_goal value = 2 compare = equals } VAL_military_target_secured = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_military_expired } } set_country_flag = VAL_military_success VAL_military_settle = yes }
  else_if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_military_expired } if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_military_goal value = 1 compare = equals } VAL_military_target_secured = yes } set_country_flag = VAL_military_success } VAL_military_settle = yes }
 }
}

VAL_record_advisor_conflict = {
 VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
 save_global_event_target_as = VAL_advisor_customer

 VAL = { set_country_flag = { flag = VAL_advisor_contract_active days = 70 } }
}
VAL_reconcile_advisor_conflict = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_advisor_contract_active }
  if = { limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { event_target:VAL_advisor_customer = { exists = yes has_capitulated = no has_war_with = event_target:VAL_advisor_enemy NOT = { has_war_with = VAL } } } } }
   event_target:VAL_advisor_customer = { remove_ideas = VAL_paid_military_advisors }
   remove_ideas = VAL_advisors_income
   clr_country_flag = VAL_advisor_contract_active
  }
 }
}

# COUNTRY buyer: payment requires current consent and an unused slot.
VAL_accept_quarterly_order_1 = {
 save_global_event_target_as = VAL_order_1_partner
 set_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_supply_active
 ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
 VAL = {
  ADISCORD_economy_initialize_country = yes
  add_political_power = -100
  set_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly
  set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_completed value = 0 }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_advance value = 0 }
  set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 1 }
  clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarter_paid
  clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
  clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
  activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
  add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 60 }
 }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 1 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 25000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 500 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 500 } } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 2 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 50000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 1000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 1000 } } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 3 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 100000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 2250 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 2250 } } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 4 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_quantity value = 200000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_price value = 50000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_remaining value = 50000 } } }
 VAL = { VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes }
 VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes
 VAL_close_order_offer = yes
}

VAL_order_1_cancel = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly }
  event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = {
   clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_supply_active
   save_event_target_as = VAL_quarterly_notice_partner
  }
  country_event = { id = val_contract.421 }
  clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly
  clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarter_paid
 }
 VAL_order_1_legacy_cancel = yes
}
VAL_order_1_dispatch = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly }
  if = {
   limit = { VAL_order_1_can_dispatch = yes }
   VAL_order_1_settle_payment = yes
   send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_order_1_quantity target = event_target:VAL_order_1_partner }
   add_to_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_completed value = 1 }
   set_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarter_paid
  }
 }
 else = { VAL_order_1_legacy_dispatch = yes }
}
# Timeout callbacks only mark expiry. The country daily hook advances the cycle.
VAL_order_1_quarterly_reconcile = {
 if = {
  limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { VAL_order_partner_valid = yes } } } }
  VAL_order_1_cancel = yes
 }
 else_if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired }
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarter_paid }
   if = {
    limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_completed value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
    event_target:VAL_order_1_partner = { clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_supply_active VAL_contract_record_success = yes save_event_target_as = VAL_quarterly_notice_partner }
    set_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 }
    clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly
    remove_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
    country_event = { id = val_contract.420 }
    VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes
   }
   else = {
    activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
    add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 60 }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = -15 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_completed value = 3 compare = equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = 5 } }
   }
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarter_paid
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used } }
   set_country_flag = VAL_order_1_grace_used
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_1_expired
   activate_mission = VAL_order_1_deadline
   add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_1_deadline days = -15 }
  }
  else = { VAL_order_1_cancel = yes }
 }
}
VAL_order_1_reconcile = {
 if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_1_quarterly } VAL_order_1_quarterly_reconcile = yes }
 else = { VAL_order_1_legacy_reconcile = yes }
}

 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 3 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_quantity value = 100000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_price value = 2250 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 2250 } } }
 if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_quote_tier value = 4 compare = equals } } VAL = { set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_quantity value = 200000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_price value = 50000 } set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_remaining value = 50000 } } }
 VAL = { VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes }
 VAL_begin_partner_contract_year = yes

   send_equipment = { equipment = infantry_equipment amount = VAL_order_2_quantity target = event_target:VAL_order_2_partner }

# Timeout callbacks only mark expiry. The country daily hook advances the cycle.
VAL_order_2_quarterly_reconcile = {
 if = {
  limit = { OR = { has_capitulated = yes NOT = { event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { VAL_order_partner_valid = yes } } } }
  VAL_order_2_cancel = yes
 }
 else_if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_expired }
  if = {
   limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_quarter_paid }
   if = {
    limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_completed value = 4 compare = greater_than_or_equals } }
    event_target:VAL_order_2_partner = { clr_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_supply_active VAL_contract_record_success = yes save_event_target_as = VAL_quarterly_notice_partner }
    set_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_state value = 0 }
    clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_quarterly
    remove_mission = VAL_order_2_deadline
    country_event = { id = val_contract.420 }
    VAL_refresh_order_summary = yes
   }
   else = {
    activate_mission = VAL_order_2_deadline
    add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_2_deadline days = 60 }
    if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_grace_used } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_2_deadline days = -15 } }
    if = { limit = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_2_completed value = 3 compare = equals } } add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_2_deadline days = 5 } }
   }
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_quarter_paid
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_grace_used
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_expired
  }
  else_if = {
   limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_grace_used } }
   set_country_flag = VAL_order_2_grace_used
   clr_country_flag = VAL_order_2_expired
   activate_mission = VAL_order_2_deadline
   add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = VAL_order_2_deadline days = -15 }
  }
  else = { VAL_order_2_cancel = yes }
 }
}
VAL_order_2_reconcile = {
 if = { limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_order_2_quarterly } VAL_order_2_quarterly_reconcile = yes }
 else = { VAL_order_2_legacy_reconcile = yes }
}

VAL_accept_order = {
 if = {
  limit = { has_country_flag = VAL_quarterly_offer }
  if = {
   limit = { VAL_order_can_accept = yes }
   if = { limit = { VAL = { NOT = { check_variable = { var = VAL_order_1_state value = 0 compare = greater_than } } NOT = { has_idea = VAL_export_income_1 } } } VAL_accept_quarterly_order_1 = yes }
   else = { VAL_accept_quarterly_order_2 = yes }
  }
 }
 else = { VAL_accept_legacy_order = yes }
}
